# AGENTS.md

This file provides guidance to coding agents (ZCode and other AGENTS.md-aware tools) when working with code in this repository.

> **How to read this file (2026-10-13 split)**: this file carries **rulings** — compact cards: the invariant, the why in one clause, the test to run, and a wiki pointer. Incident narratives, mechanism walkthroughs and decision history live in the **GitHub wiki** (`browsa.wiki.git`; local mirror `/root/workspace/browsa-wiki/`, en/zh authoritative, five more languages are snapshots). Each card names its wiki page. Conflict rule unchanged: **AGENTS.md 裁决 > wiki 解释** — if the wiki disagrees, fix the wiki. Nothing was deleted into thin air: the pre-split full text is in this repo's git history.

## Commands

```bash
npm test                        # run all tests (Node test runner, no build needed)
node --test test/foo.test.mjs   # run a single test file
npm run package                 # build versioned zip for distribution (browsa-vX.Y.Z.zip)
npm run build                   # bundle vendor libs via esbuild (only needed after changing build/build.mjs)
bash check-compat.sh            # static compatibility check
```

**Memory constraint for tests**: the dev box is a 2-CPU/4GB VPS and `npm test` runs 141 files (≈43 jsdom-heavy; 1810 assertions as of 2026-10-17 — `ls test/*.test.mjs` is the authoritative count, not this sentence). The script caps `--test-concurrency=2`; on a 4GB box prefer `--test-concurrency=1`. When adding mock data to tests, keep buffers small — a single 44MB mock blob (once the norm in the ASR sidepanel tests) plus parallel jsdom children can OOM the box mid-run (real recurrence, 2026-08). Never add tests that allocate tens of MB per case without an explicit reason, and don't silently reintroduce `new Uint8Array(44 * 1024 * 1024)`-style literals. Prefer pure-function tests (no DOM, no big blobs).

When bumping a version, update both `manifest.json` and `package.json` — `check-compat.sh` now fails on a mismatch (plus locale-key parity and referenced-vendor existence).

## Optimization sweep rulings (2026-10-17 — do not regress)

Whole-repo audit pass. Full narrative: wiki **Architecture → "The 2026-10-17 whole-repo optimization sweep"**. The cards below are the KEEP-IT constraints.

- **Per-turn state ownership**：`initStreamState` 返回回合状态；`chat-handler` 所有 per-tab 清理/清场/DONE 必须过 `ownsStream()` / `ownsController()`。同一 tabId 的第二个回合会覆盖共享槽位，旧回合的 `finally` 曾删掉新回合的取消句柄/idle 重置器/累计文本。Hermes 用本回合的 `ownRunRecord`。Tests: `chat-turn-ownership`。
- **SSE reader 必须 finally cancel**（`openSseStream`）；行式 SSE 传输单点 `lib/sse-reader.js`；`event:` 名只经 `sseEventName()`。Tests: `llm-client-reader-cancel`、`sse-reader`。
- **MAIN-world 站点脚本共享 `site-common.js`**：manifest 必须把它排在每个 `-content-script.js` 之前（顺序有测试）；站点脚本里的 `relayToHost` 是懒解析转发壳，勿再复制线格式。
- **存储**：`browsa.agentSessionKeys` 索引界定 session-store 扫描，全量 `get(null)` 只剩一次性迁移；`SESSION_DIGEST_CAP = 16_000`。`getSiteCache(tabId, url)` 按 host 选源并删 stale。
- **原会话被删 → `rescueOrphanedTurn` 重建**（抽屉显示「Recovered turn (…)」）。**禁止回退 live append**（跨会话泄漏），也禁止丢弃。Tests: `lib-persist-turn-routing`。
- **气泡加宽用 `.has-live-diagram`**（渲染器 `markBubbleWide` 盖章），CSS 禁止回 `.msg.assistant:has(...)`。Tests: `render-contract-lockstep`。
- **reader 注入走 `executeScript({files})`**，禁止 `eval`（页面 CSP 会拦，reader 静默退化成 DOM 树）。Tests: `readability-injector`。
- **CSP**：`connect-src` 的 `ws://` 只留 loopback（`wss://*` 给远程 gateway）。**六个 build 补丁 vendor 必须精确版本**（`check-compat.sh` 断言）：katex/molstar/@rdkit/docling/pdf-inspector/pdfjs。
- **`package.mjs` 是 allowlist**（manifest 引用 + 固定运行根 + `LICENSE`）；`.md`/`.log`/`.test.mjs` 不收；生成后有独立 verifier。**`docs/assets` 只收被引用的文件**（`docs-home.test.mjs` 断言，未引用即红）。
- **平台 Cookie 读取单点** `readPlatformCookie`（youtube/bilibili 白名单）。Tests: `security-contracts`。
- **构建可复现**：vendor 源全在 `devDependencies`，缺源即失败；`build/entries/marked-entry.mjs` 保住 marked 的可调用 default 契约。**vendor 重建单独走 PR + 真机门**。
- **`applyI18n` 不毁子节点**（有元素子节点时只替换首个文本节点）；**`composer-state` 的 debounce 只由测试注入**。
- **测试基建**：`test/helpers/chrome-mock.mjs` 的 `makeStorageArea(initial, {clone})` 是唯一实现，勿再在测试文件里手写 storage 桩。

## Docs sync convention (user-visible changes, 2026-09-20)

**Any user-visible change — feature, behavior, capability — syncs the public docs in the SAME PR**, without waiting to be asked. The rule was made explicit after the Office-document attach landed with its README/docs edits already bundled (2026-09-20, the intended shape) and a follow-up scan caught one missed sentence — that scan-then-fix pass is the routine to repeat:

1. **README 七语族**（2026-10-01 起；**2026-10-11 起默认=中文**——CN 推广主战场 + 入群入口在 zh README，en 改名 `README.en.md`）：`README.md`（zh）+ `README.en.md`（EN）仍是**逐 PR 权威**，sections aligned one-to-one (EN and zh are semantic mirrors, not literal translations; zh carries no English jargon)；`README.{ja,ko,es,pt-BR,ru}.md` 为 AI 初翻（2026-10-01 子代理产出），顶部互链切换行是唯一跨语导航。**当轮只保证 en/zh 同步**；五语 best-effort——滞后可接受，功能版/大版本时集中回同步（批改五语时用子代理并行、结构校验：标题数/表格行/代码块字节一致）。The recurring touchpoints for attach/extraction features: the Highlights bullets (~line 50), the 「你在读 / What browsa sends」attach table (~line 205), and the mode-less auto-attach sentence in the attach walkthrough (~line 203).
2. **GitHub Pages 落地页七语**（2026-10-01 起）：`docs/index.html`（zh）+ `docs/{en,ja,ko,es,pt-br,ru}/index.html`，共享 `../site.css`/`../site.js`，页首 `.md-header__langs` 七语导航块是**逐字节统一的 markup**（当前语 `<strong>`）——新增语言页必须复用同款。feature/privacy lists mirror README claims. **Exempt**: the demo hero mocks (the 📎 已附加… / Attached: … lines inside the fake panel scenes) are illustrative screenshots of specific scenarios, NOT a feature inventory — a new feature does not require re-staging them. **guide 保持 en/zh 双语**（9 页深度内容，判定：CWS listing 与各语落地页均链 `../en/guide/`；字段有需求再扩）；privacy.html 同样保持 en。en/zh 权威逐 PR，五语落地页 best-effort 回同步。
3. **Screenshots/banners/GIF/视频**（2026-09-22 用户明令：每次更新必须**逐一考虑**这四类动态素材，不许只想到截图；2026-10-01 明确：UI/UX 改动未涉及视频/GIF 时可不更新）——(a) guide 真机截图 `docs/assets/guide/panel-*.png`：需要更新时从 dev-preview 同尺寸重摄；(b) `dev-preview/banners/*.html` → `node dev-preview/banners/render.mjs`；(c) README 的 **demo GIF** 与官网 **promo mp4**（同一素材管线 `.agents/skills/promo-video`：现役 v7 为真实原生 UI/交互采集 → 冻结截图 → Remotion/ffmpeg；七语四章主片 68.3s，GIF32.4s 独立剪辑。当前命令与权威约束见该 skill 及 references/production-v7.md；随产品变化按 references/maintenance.md 判断是否更新及完整/局部范围。旧 v2/v3/v4/v6 仅作历史回溯；演示/实际模型素材与编辑节奏须区分，不是在线性能或实际写文件证明）。**先判断各素材的场景、操作和功能表达是否受影响，再判断各自交付分辨率下是否可辨**；视频/GIF 未涉及改动，或仅有不影响演示操作、结果、产品识别与理解的周边细节差异，可保留并记录原因，留到下次宣传片升级同步。片中点击位置、字幕步骤、渲染结果、核心外观或能力表述失真时，更新受影响镜头；用户明确要求刷新时按其范围执行。同源不等于 GIF 与视频必须一起更新，各按实际镜头独立判断，截图和 banner 也单独判断；视频/GIF 保留不免除指南操作入口和可辨截图同步。保留媒体时保留冻结输入和发布链接，无需启动采集/渲染。GIF 内容确实改变**必须换文件名**（demo-vN 递增，否则 camo/浏览器缓存永远旧图），同步受影响语言的引用。每类素材的更新/保留依据写进交付说明，不许 silently 跳过。Store-side screenshots live in `store-assets/` (gitignored) and are governed by the cws-listing skill, not this repo flow；CWS listing 嵌的 YouTube 宣传视频重拍后由用户手动换源。
4. **AGENTS.md** — architecture-level records for anything a future agent must not misinterpret (new pipeline, new vendor, new message type). If a decision closes a door (e.g. "deliberately NOT pre-warmed", "techniques portable, signature features not"), write the why down — un-recorded whys get re-litigated.
5. **GitHub wiki**（2026-10-01 起，`browsa.wiki.git` 独立仓库，本地镜像 `/root/workspace/browsa-wiki/`）— **七语**架构解说层（Architecture / Rendering-Pipeline / Storage-Model / Providers-and-Agents / ASR-and-Video-Analysis / Security-Model / Design-Decisions / Contributing / Home，每页 `-zh/-ja/-ko/-es/-pt/-ru` 孪生 + `_Sidebar` 七语区；页首语言行当前语加粗）。**权威模型：en/zh 逐 PR 更新；五语是 AI 初翻快照**（每页页脚注明权威版指针与同步日期）——架构级变更**不要求**逐 PR 回同步五语，功能版/大版本集中回同步。**wiki 解释、AGENTS.md 裁决**：两者冲突时以后者为准并回头修 wiki 页。架构级变更若存在对应 wiki 页，同 PR 内更新 `/root/workspace/browsa-wiki/` 的 en/zh 源文件并 push wiki 仓库（推前核对 git log 链 = 远端 HEAD，绝不盲推/force）。

## Architecture

**Chrome MV3 extension** — side panel chat UI. No build step is required for the JS source; all extension files are loaded directly by Chrome.

### Message flow

```
page content scripts  →  chrome.runtime.sendMessage  →  background.js (SW)
                                                              ↓
sidepanel.js  ←  navPort (browsa-nav long-lived port)  ←  background.js
sidepanel.js  ←  streamPort (browsa-chat, opened fresh per turn)  ←  background.js
sidepanel.js  ←  subChatPort (browsa-subchat, opened fresh per send)  ←  background.js
```

- **`background.js`** is the service worker and single message router. All `chrome.runtime.onMessage` handling flows through one `handle()` switch — still the single exported dispatcher (tests import it); the biggest case bodies live in `lib/handlers/`: `chat-handler.js` (`CHAT`), `subchat-handler.js` (`SUBCHAT`/`SUBCHAT_ABORT`), `attach-confirm-handler.js` (five deferred-storage confirm cases, routed via one `ATTACH_CONFIRM_TYPES.has()` check), `mermaid-repair.js` (`REPAIR_MERMAID` — one-shot apiStyle-dispatched provider call; never enters chat history; the panel re-validates with a local mermaid parse before swapping the error card), `session-handler.js` (all 8 session-drawer cases, thin pass-throughs to storage), `provider-resolver.js` (shared `resolveProvider`/`resolveInferenceParams`), `agent-stream-session.js`, `prompt-assembly.js`, `site-cache-store.js`, `approval-relay.js`, `attach-*` family. `lib/sidepanel/media-headers.js` holds the shared ASR DNR header-injection recipe. `ATTACH_PAGE` and small cases stay inline (SITE_CACHES registry 外迁 site-cache-store.js 后 case 依赖变显式 import；同批抽了 relaySelectionAction / runInVideoTab / siteCacheCtx 三个 file-local helper，xhsXhrCache 仍留 background 因推送要摸 navPorts).
- **Rulings — do not re-propose** (wiki: Design-Decisions table + ADR-0001): **Media download UI REMOVED 2026-09-05** — headers on `chrome.downloads` requests are not reliably injectable (Chrome 「无法从网站上提取文件」) + YouTube PO-token anti-bot; out-of-core-loop feature hostage to third-party CDNs. The ASR/精读 pipelines (feeding the MODEL media) keep their own fetch machinery + `media-headers.js` — unaffected. **tabCapture「边播边录」ASR-fallback REJECTED 2026-09-28** — paused/skipped segments are silence, an unplayed video can't be transcribed; `tabCapture`/`offscreen` were never added to the manifest.
- ⚠ **`LOAD_SESSION` 约定**：`storage.loadSession` 未命中返回 **-1**（0 是合法空会话长度）；调用侧只读内层 `res.data.ok`（混层 `!res?.ok && !res?.data?.ok` 曾让失败分支永不命中 → 点开已删会话假成功）。
- **`agent-stream-session.js` (C4)**：`stopHermesRun` 是 Hermes `/stop` 的唯一实现（STREAM_ABORT / chat idle-stop / subchat SUBCHAT_ABORT 三处调用）；审批/澄清 pending 条目一律走 `agentPendingEntry` 工厂——**条目形状即 approval-relay 的分派接口，字段名勿改**（2026-10-15 并发审批堆叠起条目**追加** `data` 显示载荷——STREAM_PEEK 补挂卡片要用，relay 读的既有字段不变）；键生成/清扫走同文件 `agentPendingKey(scope)`/`sweepAgentPending(map, scope)`；闭包外壳与 chunk 推送名留在两个 handler（subchat.test.mjs 的 lockstep pin 锁着源码形状，改代码必改 pin）。**`prompt-assembly.js` (C5)**：CAPABILITY_HINTS/CHOICE_REQUEST_HINT 单份，/prompt 检视器显示=发送（镜像缺块曾出过事故）。**`approval-relay.js`**：per-kind relay 唯一 owner。**pending map 按 reqKey 堆叠（2026-10-15，勿退回 tabId/subId 单槽）**：曾按 tabId/subId 单槽键控——同一 surface 的第二个并发审批事件 set 覆盖第一条（requestId 永久丢失、agent 干等），面板 `removeAgentCards()` 又删掉旧卡，还有窄竞态：应答在途时槽位被覆盖，给 A 的「允许」错配到 B 的 requestId。现在每条 pending 键=`agentPendingKey(scope)`（`<tabId|subId>::<seq>`），APPROVAL/CLARIFY chunk 带 `reqKey`，卡片 dataset 也有，RESPOND 按 reqKey 精确路由；**答成即删（两侧同语义，失败保留供重试，卡片同样失败不销毁）**；回合收尾 finally/abort 用 `sweepAgentPending` 按 scope 清扫；面板侧卡片**堆叠**（按 reqKey 去重、按插入序排在最后一张卡后），`removeAgentCards` 只剩回合收尾用；STREAM_PEEK 的 in-flight 分支返回 `pendingAgentCards`（该 scope 名下未决条目），resume 路径据此补挂——重开面板不再出现「流停在等审批却没有卡可答」。行为地板：approval-clarify（reqKey 路由+答成删+PEEK 补挂+创建点 pin）与 lib-detail-thread（两事件→两卡→各答各的，变异验证）双侧；hermes clarify body 两条路径都 `String(response ?? '')` 规范化（background 曾发裸 null）。
- **`lib/llm-client.js`** is the wire-protocol layer (`chatStream` / `runsApiStream` / `responsesStream` / `anthropicStream` + apiStyle-aware `ping` — see "Provider API styles" below). 四条流共用一个 `openSseStream()` 骨架（C1：fetch/错误分类/预算协商/abort/切分/收尾），每协议只留 `buildRequest` + 纯事件解析；`<thinking>` 开合状态机单份。**`thinking: 'inline'|'omit'` option (default `'omit'`)**：'inline' 把推理过程包成**一个**内联 `<thinking>…</thinking>` 块；`chatStream` 另解析 DeepSeek 式 `reasoning_content`（含 `reasoning` 别名）。**ONLY chat-handler 和 subchat-handler 传 `thinking:'inline'`**（主聊天+追问卡要活折叠块）；其余消费者（attach-summarizer / mermaid-repair / selection-explain / agentic-extract）保持 omit——输出必须是干净文本，**勿翻默认**。
- **Hermes runs reasoning truth (ADR-0004, 勿「修」)**：`/v1/runs` 唯一的 reasoning 载体 `reasoning.available` 是步骤**答案**的 ≤500 字 replay（step 末尾发出），单步轮与已流式文本逐字节相同——llm-client 的 echo guard 丢弃它是正确的，显示它会复读答案。agent 实时推理 delta 不达任何 API 面（gateway `_create_agent` 不订阅 reasoning_callback）；真要暴露需 Hermes 侧补丁 + browsa 侧合并（叙事见 wiki Providers-and-Agents）。compat 层同理无 reasoning 面 → SUBCHAT 走 `runsApiStream` 专用 per-subId 会话（`subchatHermesSessions`，绝不用主聊天的存储会话）；abort 经 `subChatRunIds` 打服务端 `/v1/runs/{id}/stop`。
- The shared stream/approval state Maps (`streamPorts`, `streamState`, `chatControllers`, `idleTimerResetters`, `activeRunIds`, `pendingApprovals`, `pendingClarifications`, `subChatControllers`, `subChatPorts`) plus `pushChunk`/`pushSubChatChunk`/`initStreamState`/`appendToStreamState`/`clearStreamState` live in `lib/state.js` — `background.js` re-exports them, so tests importing from `../background.js` get the same Map instances.
- **Durable in-flight turn record + SW-death salvage (2026-10-13)** — 流式中途 SW 被 Chrome 杀掉时，回合不再无声蒸发：`lib/state.js` 把每个活回合镜像进 `chrome.storage.session`（索引 `browsa.inflightTurns` + 每 tab `browsa.inflightTurn.<tabId>`；delta 防抖 1s 落盘、acc 尾部截 512KB——durable 副本只为收尸，终止路径落全文）；`salvageDurableTurns()` 在每次 SW 醒来把记录补成 `{ interrupted: true }` 条目（`persistTurnEntry` 的 `fromDurable` 传 origin/bg——不传会把后台流收尸进 live history，正是 bg 路由要防的跨会话泄漏）；STREAM_PEEK 在答「不在流式中」前 await 同一个 memoized sweep，以**每 tab 读一次**的 `recovered: true` 让面板重渲一次；收尸仅限非空（首 delta 前死无可收）。**完成窗口竞态（2026-10-14 字段报告：流式中关面板→重开看不到内容）**：turn 在面板重建窗口内完成——DONE 落库在 init renderHistory 读历史**之后**、PEEK 在 clearStreamState **之后**——回复在库却两头没接住（真实历史大、渲染慢，窗口以秒计；thinking 期重开没事是因为 turn 还在跑、走 resume）。PEEK 的 not-in-flight 分支现在**非消费地**查 terminalTombstone（`peekTerminalTombstone`，take 的语义不变），命中回 `{inFlight:false, justFinished:true}`，面板与 recovered 同路重渲一次。**僵尸不变量**：面板已关时到来的真实错误必须 tombstone ERROR + clear streamState（否则状态挂到 10 分钟 GC，重开面板会去续一条死流永远转圈）；活面板的错误走响应通道（不 tombstone，避免双显）+ onSend `!res.ok` 发 STREAM_RELEASE。Tests: `test/inflight-turn-salvage.test.mjs`。叙事：wiki Architecture「SW-death salvage」。
- The 9 site content-script push messages (`YOUTUBE_DATA`/`JUEJIN_ARTICLE`/etc.) route through one generic case driven by `SITE_MESSAGE_MAP` (`{msgType: {site, field}}` table next to `SITE_CACHES`) — no per-site case blocks.
- **YouTube transcript acquisition vs POT** — 阶梯固定勿乱动：**2b2 InnerTube mobile clients 先行**（`ANDROID` 20.10.38 → `ANDROID_VR` 1.60.19 → `IOS` 20.10.3 依序；`contentCheckOk/racyCheckOk:true`、fresh `{context:{client}}` 不得从 `INNERTUBE_CONTEXT` 展开、`prettyPrint=false`）——mobile `/player` 的字幕 URL 可被纯 fetch 拉到真内容；`exp=xpe` token-gated track **处处过滤**（含 `fetchTranscript`，200+空 regardless cookies）；`&fmt=json3` 只在 mobile URL 上剥了再接（「fmt 破坏签名」说的是 WEB pot URL）；**2c CC 触发**仅作 2b2 之后的兜底（驱动播放器自己的字幕模块让授权请求过 interceptor，结束后恢复用户 CC 状态——闪一下不可避免，即授权抓取本体，用户已接受）；`__browsaCaptionTriggerEmpty` 按 videoId 负缓存（仅当前视频的 tracklist-empty 判定可写；未初始化播放器不毒缓存；mobile 零 track 即权威无字幕判定直接写缓存）。Bilibili 全不需要（`/x/player/wbi/v2` 主动拉，与 CC 开关无关）。**活动页形态（2026-10-19 用户报告）**：`/festival/…?bvid=BV…` 等活动页把 bvid 放在查询参数里且 `__INITIAL_STATE__` 无 `videoData`（实测 `bvid` 字段为 null）——`activeFetchBilibiliVideo` 的 bvid 提取必须走 `bilibiliBvidFromUrl`（`/video/` 路径 → `?bvid=` 查询参数），缺失即抛 `'no bvid on page'`、字幕链路整体不启动。**修完提取后用户实测仍失败——真正的第一道门在 site-fallbacks**：`tryBilibiliActiveFallback` 的 URL 门 `bilibili\.com\/video\/` 把活动页整个拒掉（门不放行，提取修复就是死代码），已放宽到 `/video/`、`/festival/`、`/blackboard/`、任何带 `?bvid=`/`?aid=` 的 bilibili 页；YouTube 同理由 `watch?` 放宽到 `shorts/live/embed/v`。回归测试走真 `handle()`（ATTACH_PAGE → 门 → 注入 → 字幕进 `ctx.text`，断言不是通用页面 dump）。**同日补齐同族缺口**：YouTube videoId 提取认 `/shorts/`、`/live/`、`/embed/`、`/v/` 路径（此前只认 `?v=`，Shorts 页第一步就 return null；embed 播放列表 `videoseries` 拒掉），bilibili 认 `?aid=`（view API 按 aid 查询，player.html 嵌入页形态）与 `?page=` 分 P 参数。叙事：wiki ASR-and-Video-Analysis。
- **Xiaoyuzhou podcast ASR**：`ctx.mode === 'xiaoyuzhou'`（恒 `noTranscript:true`）→ `buildAsrPendingCtx` 第三分支：**无 executeScript 探针**——音频取 interceptor 缓存的 `mediaUrl`（enclosure），回退 SW 拉 `og:audio`；两路都必须过 `isXiaoyuzhouMediaUrl` 白名单（页面可控字段）。双失败 → null → 纯附加 + `noTranscriptHint`（ASR 开着但 ctx 为 null 时也提示）。面板侧：无 DNR/无 Referer/无 403 自愈；`videoAssetId` = `xy-<eid>` **绝不能空串**（方舟文件缓存键，空串=全播客共用一个缓存槽）；无模式卡；ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM **不盖 `videoSrc`**（纯音频无 seek 目标）。叙事：wiki ASR-and-Video-Analysis。
- **Deferred-storage attach family (2026-09-20 pass)**：五个确认类 case 的存储配方单点在 `lib/handlers/attach-store.js` `storeAttachment()`（screenshot `summarize:false`；ASR 传 `summarizeText` 防阈值误判；office ext 缺省时 header 不带格式注）；`lib/attach-modes.js` 一张表（`ATTACH_MODE_CAPS` + `DEFERRED_HANDOFFS`）供 background ATTACH_PAGE 与 attach-orchestrator 共读——**asr-pending 刻意不是表行**（它的 handoff 是行为性的，需要 `all.asr` 设置）。行为 pin 在 `test/attach-pdf-confirm.test.mjs` + `attach-office-confirm.test.mjs`（走真 `handle()`）。
- **Site byte-images ride the attach entry (2026-10-11 XHS fix)**：MAIN-world 抓到的站点字节图（`ctx.imageBase64List`）在 ATTACH_PAGE 尾部经 `interleaveImageParts` 进条目的 `[text, ...image_url]` 无锚形状（合成文本只有计数行，无 `[图N]` 锚）。**勿在 `buildMessages` 找请求侧图片出口**——其 v0.20 的 `pageContext`/`withImage` 参数自 v0.21 起就是死代码且已物理删除。预算制上限：候选 18 / `SITE_IMAGES_BUDGET_CHARS` 4.5M，权威裁剪是 SW 侧纯函数 `capFetchedImages`（lib/image-budget.js）；**agent turn 同日 8→18 / 3M→4.5M，前提是 agent-bridge ≥0.8.0（16MB body）——升级顺序桥在先**，旧桥由 `bridgeStream` 的 400 一次性重试降级为「前 8 张 + 模型面短缺点名」；squilla 网关另有 ≤16 张/≤5MB 合同（upstream 0.5.6，2026-10-16 对齐；被砍张数出模型面点名，不静默）；文章配图与站点字节图共用同一组常量（lib/page-images.js 不再有独立 MAX_IMAGES）。接线测试地板：`attach-site-images`（存储半）+ `chat-rebuild-channels` Channel 0（线上半）+ `site-synthesizers`（字节随行 pin）——**改任一侧必跑对面**。RESIDUAL：xhscdn 常规 fetch 假定 CDN 发 ACAO（未真机验证）；若 CORS 收紧，下一杠杆是 SW 侧 fetch + media-headers DNR；诊断看 SW console 的 `N site images` 与页面 `browsa[xhs]: fetched N/M`。叙事：wiki Architecture「2026-10-11 site byte-images incident」。
- **Zhihu figures ride the [图N] pipeline (2026-10-15 回归修复)**：#223 的知乎全文 fallback 曾用 `htmlToText` 把 API HTML 剥成纯文本——图片 URL 一步丢失，合成模式 `zhihu` 又不在 caps 表里（用户报告「知乎专栏图片 attach 不上去」）。现在 `zhihu-content-script.js` 的 `htmlToArticleText` 把 content HTML **保序**转成带 `![alt](url)` 的文本（`data-actualsrc`/`data-original` 优先于 80px `src`、跳 `noscript` 孪生与 `data:`/`blob:` 占位、alt 去 markdown 破坏字符），`ATTACH_MODE_CAPS.zhihu = { inlineImages: true }` 接通 reader/auto 同一条 `inlinePageImages` 管线（被动拦截与主动 fallback 两路共用该转换器）。**zhimg.com 的门是「不像浏览器 或 无站内 Referer」二选一（2026-10-15 同 IP 真值表：curl UA 无/伪造 Referer 403、curl UA + 知乎 Referer 200、浏览器 UA 无 Referer 也 200——真扩展 SW 裸 fetch 靠浏览器特征启发式现况可过）**，显式注入知乎 Referer 是把合同钉死、不赌启发式不收紧（收紧即静默降级 fail-open）；background 的 inlineImages 分支命中 `lib/page-images.js` 的 `IMAGE_CDN_REFERER_GATES` 门表时经 `registerPageImageReferer`（lib/sidepanel/media-headers.js，**固定 ruleId 9000001**，initiatorDomains=扩展自身，finally 移除）挂 DNR session 规则注入站内 Referer。给门表加新图床前必须同法实测该 CDN 的 Referer 行为。**姊妹缺口未接**（同形状、未报告未验证，勿当回归）：juejin `markContent` / dedao / geektime / xueqiu 合成模式同样没有 inlineImages 行——接之前先验各自图床。测试地板（变异验证过）：`attach-site-images`（handle() 级三例：图钉+门注册清理 / 非门域不碰 DNR / 下载全失败 fail-open 且规则不泄漏）+ **同文件「知乎链路」节（2026-10-15 用户令「写上测试用例把底兜住」后新增的地板层）：jsdom 驱动真实 `activeZhihuFetch`/`extractZhihuArticle`（只 stub 网络）产出喂真实 ATTACH_PAGE + 被动路径 toText 绑定的源码 lockstep pin**——教训：落库桩测试喂的是手工提取结果，提取侧把图剥掉时照样绿（本回归恰好从桩层之下漏过）；**站点能力的链条必须有只 stub 网络的链路级测试** + `site-content-scripts`（转换器 jsdom 三例）+ `lib-page-images`（门表）+ `lib-attach-modes`（caps 集合）。Tests: 同左。
- **Site-figure sibling batch (2026-10-15 同日姊妹批，同审计接通)**：juejin / geektime / xueqiu 三家接上同一条 [图N] 管线（caps 行 + 提取侧修图）。**juejin** 只差 caps 行——`mark_content` 原生 Markdown，图是 `pX-xtjj-sign.byteimg.com` 签名 URL（签名即鉴权，实测不挑 UA/Referer，无需 DNR 门）。**geektime** 与知乎回归同形（`textContent` 剥图）——`geektime-content-script.js` 升级为同款 `htmlToArticleText`（自包含拷贝：MAIN world 不能 import），fetch/XHR 两处绑定 + 源码 lockstep pin（文件内不得残留 `htmlToText`）；图 URL 是 `//static001.geekbang.org` 协议相对，`collectMarkdownImages` 按 baseUrl 绝对化。**xueqiu** 帖子图两个来源：内联 `<img src>` 原位转 markdown + `image_info_list[].filename`（RSSHub 同字段 → `xqimg.imedao.com`）——CDN 对真图未实测（匿名应答正常、无门禁迹象），fail-open 兜底故不加门；真机样本来后若 403 再入门表。**dedao 未接（已知缺口，非回归）**：content 字段原样透传（HTML 不剥不转）——图最多以 `<img>` 标签文字存在且带噪声；修复需要真实付费内容样本定 HTML 形状（勿盲改，等样本）。测试：`attach-site-images` 三家 wiring（真实推送→缓存→落库）+ `site-content-scripts`（geektime 转换器/绑定 pin、xueqiuPostText）+ `lib-attach-modes`（caps 集合）；三家变异验证过（拆行/剥图各红 5）。图床实测：byteimg/geekbang 200 无门；xqimg 未验。
- **Same-day incident-class audit (2026-10-11)**：消息通道图全清（`NAV_HELLO_ACK` 是面板不读的 fire-and-forget ack，无害）；`GET_XHS_NOTE` 已无生产发送者但**是 background-handler.test.mjs 安全 pin 的测试缝（tabId 源自 sender.tab.id）——勿删**（代码内注释已写明该角色）；选区 markdown 曾写无消费者的 `ctx.selectedFormat`，已改 `ctx.format` 并 pin 在 `test/attach-selection-markdown.test.mjs`（attach 族最后一个无 handle() 级接线的模式由此闭合）；三个 write-only 元数据字段（articleByline/Excerpt/SiteName）无害留任。方法教训：**任何跨层数据搬运后，重跑形状 lockstep 接线测试，别只跑单元套件**（用户明令的接线测试地板由此而来）。

**Agent-turn caps raised to match (2026-10-11, same day)**: `AGENT_TURN_MAX_IMAGES` 8→18, `AGENT_TURN_IMAGE_BUDGET_CHARS` 3M→4.5M (= `SITE_IMAGES_BUDGET_CHARS`), sized for **agent-bridge 0.8.0's 16MB body** (bridge-side relax shipped first as PR #96 / npm 0.8.0 — the pure-validation relaxation keeps the v1 FROZEN ruling intact; ORDER MATTERS: bridge first, browsa second, or old bridges 400 the whole turn). The old-bridge hazard is closed at runtime, not by docs: `bridgeStream` retries ONCE on a 400 matching /images must be an array of ≤\d+ URL strings/ with the first 8 images plus a model-facing text note (升级 agent-bridge 到 0.8.0+…), so an outdated bridge degrades to "first 8 ride, the shortfall is named" instead of failing the turn; genuine 400s don't retry. Docs claims updated: README zh/en bridge sections + guide providers zh/en (≤18, bridge ≥0.8.0, degradation note). Tests: bridge-client fallback (12 images → retry 8 + note; non-image 400 no retry), pickTurnImages hard cap renamed to the constant, bridge-client wire-bound slice test.
Standing method: after ANY refactor that moves data across a layer boundary, re-run the shape-lockstep wiring tests, not just the unit suites.

**Downstream per-provider note (2026-10-11 correction)**: the OpenSquilla main-chat turn DOES carry these bytes — `buildSquillaTurn` converts entry `image_url` parts into gateway base64 `attachments` (data: URLs only, ≤16 张 / ≤5MB each — the gateway ingest contract, upstream 0.5.6 `contracts/attachments.py`，2026-10-16 对齐最新 main：集成时镜像的 10 已被上游放宽，browsa 常量随后跟上; http-only figure URLs stay as text `[图N]` lines, the documented degradation). The data:-images knocked out by the 10-cap used to vanish silently — now counted and surfaced as a model-facing note (「另有 N 张图片超出网关附件上限…已随附 M 张」), same philosophy as agent-turn's drop note; pinned in `squilla-client.test.mjs`. **Article-figure caps unified (2026-10-11, user directive 「统一放开」)**: `lib/page-images.js` no longer has its own `MAX_IMAGES = 8` — it imports `SITE_IMAGES_MAX_COUNT` (18) and `SITE_IMAGES_BUDGET_CHARS` (4.5M) from lib/image-budget.js, the same constants as the site-byte-image pipeline (the two pipelines converge at ATTACH_PAGE's entry; two cap regimes over one entry shape had no justification). `inlinePageImages` gained a `budgetChars` option (default = the shared constant) with stop-at-first-overflow fill and `[图片]` collapse for budget-lost figures, same behavior as count-lost ones; `MAX_CANDIDATES` 16→24 (download-failure headroom for larger figure sets). Article figures are smaller (768px q0.72) so budget rarely binds before count. Tests: lib-page-images (18-count cap + budget-injection matrix, values computed from the stub's exact dataURL size).

What stays browsa-side for squilla by deliberate contract: pasted `msg.images` (the gateway attachment contract covers rendered page figures) and ALL subchat images. Tests (mutation-verified floor — reverting the contentFrom conversion turns exactly the four image-ride tests red): `test/attach-site-images.test.mjs` pins the STORAGE half through the real `handle()` for all four site paths (XHS XHR fast path incl. the meta-crash regression, XHS INITIAL_STATE poll path, X active fallback, Reddit active fallback, plus the all-fetch-fail plain-string fallback); `test/chat-rebuild-channels.test.mjs`'s "Channel 0: attach-entry image parts must reach the wire" section pins the WIRE half (an entry in the stored shape must surface its pixels in chat/completions `image_url` parts, responses `input_image`, anthropic base64 blocks, and hermes `[image]` markers — never raw base64 in the runs string history); `site-synthesizers.test.mjs` byte-carry pins. The two halves meet at the documented entry shape — a refactor that drifts either side goes red.

**Deepening pass round 2 (2026-09-20, candidates #1/#4/#5/#6/#8 — all eight review items now addressed)**:
- **#1, `lib/handlers/turn-request.js`**: the per-provider-kind request-shape ladder that handleChat spelled out FOUR times (initial build / overflow rebuild / continuation rebuild / timestamp-rewrite rebuild) is now ONE `createTurnRequest(...)` object with `prepare()` / `rebuildFrom(history)` / `continueWith(instr, priorReply)` / `rewriteWith(instr, history)` / `overflowRescuable`. chat-handler keeps the WHEN (gates with their lockstep-tested literals) and the stream dispatch; the HOW (payload shapes) lives here. The four chat-handler-local builders (`buildHermesTurn`/`buildRunsConversationHistory`/`buildResponsesInput`/`buildAnthropicMessages`) live in `lib/message-builder.js` since 2026-09-23 (C9) and are imported directly by the factory — the old injection was only there to dodge an import cycle; chat-handler re-exports them to keep its tests' import surface). **subchat-handler deliberately does NOT use this module** — its single ladder diverges on purpose (dedicated per-subId Hermes sessions, pinned by test/subchat.test.mjs's `doesNotMatch(/getOrCreateHermesSessionId/)`); do not merge it in without revisiting that pin. The lockstep test pinning "every apiStyle rebuilt" moved to the new seam (chat-handler-internals: asserts `turn.continueWith`/`turn.rewriteWith` calls + per-kind branches inside turn-request.js).
- **#4, storage.js**: `chrome.storage.session` boilerplate factored into module-private `sessionGet/Set/Remove`; the isHermes/isOpencode/isBridge reset ladder moved out of CLEAR_HISTORY into `storage.clearAllAgentSessions(providers)` — adding a fourth agent provider = session family + one flag line there.
- **渲染模块重构 #5（render.js / tool-tier.js）** — 裁决：`FENCED_RENDERERS` 是渲染器清单的唯一来源（hljs 跳过集由它派生）；`CAPABILITY_HINTS`/`AGENT_RENDER_HINT` 是**手工镜像的 PROMPT 文本**，为 KV 缓存字节稳定而刻意不自动生成，改一处必须改另一处。`finishBubble` 是收尾气泡的唯一实现（四条完成路径共用）；**videoSrc 盖章必须先于 `finishBubble`**——顺序倒置 = 视频笔记时间戳静默失效。`classifyToolTier` 在叶子模块 `tool-tier.js`，勿在卡内再复制一份。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **Rendering-Pipeline-zh「渲染模块重构 #5」**
- **#6, lib/sidepanel/history-index.js**: the hidx counter + shift-after-delete protocol moved out of sidepanel.js into this facade (`hidxAssign/Bump/Decrement/ResetTo/Current/ShiftAfter`); the three hand-rolled shift loops (single delete, undo-attach, multiselect) collapsed into `hidxShiftAfter`. `hidxDecrement` is deliberately NOT floored at 0 — the counter is a mirror, clamping would mask an unpaired decrement, and reconcileHistoryIdx exists to correct that drift (the two raw `--` sites never floored; only the old injected callback did — the facade follows the raw majority). initMultiselect lost its `decrementNextHistoryIdx` dep; history-reconcile.js stays the pure planner.
- **模块抽取 #8（attach 族 / one-shot 分发）** — 裁决：`initAttachOrchestrator` 显式解构依赖（initX 注入模型不变）；`attach-asr-pending.js` 只依赖 attach-asr 三个 helper + storage，故可零状态外迁；`stream-dispatch.js` 是 apiStyle→stream 的唯一派发点（chat/subchat/selection-explain/mermaid-repair 共用）。**会话式 agent 调用绝不传 `agent`**（那是一次性分支）；图片上限只由 `lib/image-budget.js` 拥有。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **Architecture-zh「模块抽取 #8（attach 族与 one-shot 分发）」**
- **`sidepanel.js`** connects `browsa-nav` once on init (receives NAVIGATED, XHS notes, SELECTION_ACTION). `browsa-chat` (main chat streaming) and `browsa-subchat` (detail-thread streaming, see below) are each opened **fresh per turn/send** instead — see "Port lifecycle" below for why that distinction matters. `sidepanel.js` itself is now the UI orchestrator (init/onSend/history/approval-clarify cards/screenshot crop) — the Markdown rendering pipeline, sessions drawer, message search, multiselect, and detail-thread are all split into `lib/sidepanel/*.js` modules (see "sidepanel.js module split" below) and imported back in.
- **`lib/storage.js`** wraps `chrome.storage.local`. History is a **global flat array** (not per-tab). `getAll()` reads a FIXED light-key list — `GET_ALL_KEYS()` = DEFAULTS keys minus `history` + the four options settings (`fontSize`/`fontFamily`/`sendShortcut`/`thoughtAutoCollapse`) — **not** `get(null)`: a 2026-09-23 efficiency pass found `get(null)` deserialized `history` (≤8MB parked image base64) plus every saved session on EVERY chat turn / attach / explain just to delete the heavy keys from the result afterward. CONTRACT: a NEW storage.local key that getAll consumers must see has to be added to `GET_ALL_KEYS`; keys read via targeted `chrome.storage.local.get` elsewhere (uiLang, quickbarCollapsed, composerState, pendingUpdateNotice, xhsAnchorFingerprint, …) don't belong there. Absent keys are filtered (`v !== undefined`) before spreading over DEFAULTS so a missing entry can't shadow a default. **savedSessions snapshots are verbatim history copies INCLUDING image bytes — deliberately, user decision 2026-09-23, do not re-propose stripping**: an efficiency audit flagged the unbounded per-entry bytes (≤8MB parked images each, ×50 entries) and proposed compacting images at save time; the user rejected it because a restored session's first turn then re-sends the actual pixels, keeping pixel-level follow-up questions answerable (history rides to the model VERBATIM — 2026-10-14 KV-cache ruling superseding ADR-0013's request-side compaction: provider prefix caches need byte-identical prefixes, `prepareHistoryForModel` now only strips the legacy `imagesSeen` stamp from request bodies; storage pixels are never destroyed). **Split-key layout (2026-09-30 批E)**: the snapshot bytes now live in per-session `session_<id>` keys while `savedSessions` itself is a LIGHT INDEX `[{id,name,createdAt,pinned,textDigest}]` — every session function funnels through `readSessionIndex()`, which migrates legacy single-key snapshots on the spot (bodies written BEFORE the index rewrite; idempotent). Listing/pinning/renaming touch only the index; drawer search matches names + `textDigest` (lowercased text-only digest, capped at 100K chars/session — the documented trade: content past the cap isn't content-searchable, name search unaffected); cap eviction and delete/clear remove body keys. `session_<id>` keys are targeted-read keys and deliberately NOT in GET_ALL_KEYS. `getSessionFull`/`loadSession` reassemble the pre-split session shape.
- **Saved conversation Agent identity (2026-10-01 bug fix)**: saved sessions carry `agentContextId`, `agentSessions` (the per-provider/per-bridge-endpoint ID and title-stamp map), and optional `agentHistoryBackfill` in the LIGHT index; `session_<id>` remains a verbatim history array. Current IDs are durable in the targeted-read local key `agentSessionState = {contextId, ids, legacyRestored?}`, with `storage.session` as a cache; it is deliberately NOT in `GET_ALL_KEYS`. Save/load restore history AND IDs. Clear starts a fresh context. ID getters are read-only in the sidepanel; only worker turn/mutation paths initialize context. Identity mutations share the session index lock; clear/load take session lock then history lock, never the reverse. Main Agent turns capture the context before preparation and use it for ID reads, async creation/bridge callbacks, and title stamps; the initial user append atomically checks that context under session→history locks, cancelling a setup-stage turn if the user switched before streaming started; delayed IDs update all snapshots with that origin context, including the save→load gap, without touching a different live context. Pre-fix snapshots cannot recover unrecorded server IDs: loading migrates them to a stable fresh context, and the first successful turn to each bridge/opencode/squilla thread stamps `agentSessionBackfilled_*` after sending its text history (creation alone does not consume pending backfill; Squilla uses a document upload when the full legacy payload exceeds its inline cap) (Hermes already sends conversation history). This legacy-only fallback does not change the user’s carry/new choice when switching Agents in newer conversations. Detail threads retain their separate session ownership. No UI layout/translation string change; guide screenshots, banners, GIFs and promo video each considered: their static single-session examples remain accurate, so no recapture for this identity fix.
- **Selection language ownership (2026-10-01 fix)**: the ISOLATED classic content script keeps its inline `UI_LANG_DIRS` map in lockstep with `lib/i18n.js`; language changes reload a dictionary with a generation guard and refresh existing text/title/aria without rebuilding handlers. Dynamic labels use that same dictionary. The worker creates/updates context-menu titles after reading `uiLang`, serialized across startup/install/live changes (menus persist across SW sleep; update/removeAll use callback completion to support Chrome 116–122). `lib/selection-language.js` owns seven UI-language answer/translation targets plus the existing German/French reply-language options shared by inline selection and panel right-click prompts: explicit `replyLanguage` wins; otherwise UI language applies. Preserve zh↔en Auto behavior; kana/Hangul/Cyrillic can identify matching targets for an English fallback, Latin-script short selections cannot reliably distinguish Spanish/Portuguese/English, so do not auto-flip them by guess. Backend inline requests re-read saved UI preference to avoid stale content-script language. Guide screenshots, banners, GIF and video were considered individually: existing Chinese selection screenshot and static promo scenes show the same labels; changes affect language selection/live synchronization, so no recapture needed for those examples.
- **SW wake cost (2026-09-23 efficiency pass, do not regress)**: (1) the three `chrome.webNavigation` listeners (`onHistoryStateUpdated`/`onCommitted`/`onBeforeNavigate`) register ONLY while a `browsa-nav` port is connected — `syncNavListeners()` is called from every navPorts mutation in the port handler; with `<all_urls>` host permissions, static registration cold-started the SW (644KB module parse) on every navigation/SPA pushState in every tab just to no-op on an empty `navPorts`. (2) the stream-GC alarm (`browsa-stream-gc`) exists ONLY while `streamState` is non-empty — `lib/state.js`'s `syncGcAlarm()` is called from `initStreamState`/`clearStreamState` and after `gcStreamState()`'s sweep; the old permanent 5-min alarm cold-started the SW 288×/day to sweep an empty Map. (3) the sidepanel's two biggest vendor bundles are lazy: `stream-markdown-parser.bundle.js` (388KB, only `sanitizeMermaidSvg`, loaded with the first mermaid block via `getSanitizeMermaidSvg()`) and `katex.bundle.js` (266KB, `getKatex()` in katex-worker-client — a top-level import there had silently turned render.js's own dynamic import into a no-op cache hit).
- **Response envelope**: most background responses use `{ ok: true, data: result }` on success / `{ ok: false, error, code, hint }` on thrown error — unwrap with `res.data?.ok` ONLY for handlers that themselves return an inner `{ok, ...}` object (e.g. `APPROVAL_RESPOND`/`CLARIFY_RESPOND`, which catch relay failures internally). Handlers that just `throw` on failure and return plain data otherwise (e.g. `CHAT`, `SUBCHAT`) are correctly checked via the outer `res.ok` directly — check which pattern a given case actually uses before assuming.
- **`lib/constants.js`** holds shared constants (e.g. `PAGE_CONTEXT_PREFIX`) imported by background, sidepanel, storage, and page-extractor. Content scripts run in MAIN world and cannot import ES modules, so they do not use this file.

### `sidepanel.js` module split (`lib/sidepanel/`)

Twenty-seven modules (2026-10-20 count — `ls lib/sidepanel/*.js` is authoritative; the directory also holds three `*.worker.js` scripts and the SW-consumed `media-headers.js`, which are not sidepanel imports), each imported by `sidepanel.js` — none of them import `sidepanel.js` back (avoids ES module cycles; modules that need `sidepanel.js`-owned mutable state get it via an `initX(deps)` call from `init()` instead). The newest is `detail-thread-store.js` (追问卡跨侧栏关闭的持久化, see the detail-thread section); the biggest recent extraction is `attach-orchestrator.js`: it holds the 📎 attach pipelines (`onAttachPage` / `runVideoAnalysisPipeline` / `runAudioTranscribePipeline` / `downloadAndTranscodeAudioBest` / `showAsrModeCard`, ~840 lines moved out of `sidepanel.js`), with `sidepanel.js` injecting its mutable state (`currentTabId`, `ctxRadios`, `nextHistoryIdx` via a `bumpHistoryIdx` callback) and the progress/append helpers through `initAttachOrchestrator(deps)`. **onAttachPage is a thin mode-dispatcher (2026-09-20 refactor pass, was 340 lines)**: the four deferred-storage branch bodies live in same-file functions `attachScreenshotMode(ctx, title)` / `attachPdfPending(ctx)` / `attachOfficePending(ctx)` / `attachAsrPending(ctx)` — all deps are the module-level `deps` + imports, so they move with zero closure threading; onAttachPage dispatches on `ctx.mode` inside its try (a branch throw, e.g. the ASR expired-playurl abort, still lands in the shared catch/finally that restores the attach button). The rest are described below:

- `render.js` — the Markdown/Mermaid/ECharts/Markmap/KaTeX pipeline: `renderStreamingSafe`/`renderSafe` (marked + DOMPurify), `makeStreamRenderer`, `addCodeCopyButtons`/`highlightDiffBlocks`, `renderMermaid`/`renderEcharts`/`renderMarkmap`. 裁决与不变量（机制与事故叙事见 wiki Rendering-Pipeline「Contracts & incident notes」）：
  - **围栏渲染器无按钮**（ADR-0010）：`CAPABILITY_HINTS` 教模型吐 ` ```markmap ` 等围栏，render.js 探测语言换活渲染——勿加 UI 按钮。
  - **markmap 专有坑**：`pre.replaceWith(wrapper)` 必须在 `Markmap.create()` **之前**（markmap-view 要挂载的 `<svg>` 量文字），catch 换回的是 `wrapper` 不是 `pre`；`Markmap.create()` 返回值必须接（工具条要 `mm.fit()/.rescale()`）。**`mm.rescale(t)` 是相对倍率不是绝对目标**——绝对定位一律走 `_markmapZoomBy(mm, svgEl, factor)`（目标 clamp [0.2,4] 后传 `target/current`；直接传 `currentScale*factor` 会被再乘一次变成平方，+/− 都缩小的真 bug）。当前缩放读 `svgEl.__zoom?.k`（d3-zoom 自己挂在节点上的字段），不为一次查询单独 import d3。导出复用 `_mermaidExportSvg`；ResizeObserver 走 `_observeResize`/`_chartObservers`（`renderHistory` 统一 `disposeChartObservers()`——RO 强引用 target，不摘即泄漏）。
  - **vendor 预热按需门控（2026-09-23 效率批，勿退回无条件预热）**：`preloadChartVendors()` 仅在确见过图 fence 时触发——(a) CHUNK 流里 delta 含反引号就测累计文本（fence 开头会跨 delta 劈开）、(b) DONE 重测最终文本、(c) init 空闲时按 `renderHistory` 返回的 `historyHasCharts`（`wantsChartVendors(text)` 由 `FENCED_RENDERERS` 派生，不会漂）。~7MB 解析只由真用图图的会话支付；每个 `getXxx()` 自带幂等。
  - **`renderSafe` 是 `async`**（等 KaTeX worker/阈值决策）——调用方必须 `await`；`renderStreamingSafe`（逐帧流式路径）保持同步；`makeStreamRenderer` 闭包同样 async，且重派前必须先调 `.destroy()`（RETRY / 换 DOM 身份时停掉被弃的 reveal-pacer）。
  - **块级增量流式渲染（ADR-0011）**：每帧只解析开放尾部（`findStreamingBlockBoundary` 纯函数：free fence 外的空行边界、下一行是列表项则拒绝）；display 与活 think 共用；**前缀稳定不变量**（缓冲尾部的残缺 `<think` 无换行，永不过界被提交）；DONE 仍 `renderSafe` 全量重渲兜底。Tests: `test/lib-stream-incremental.test.mjs`。
  - **活 think 折叠策略（2026-10-07）**：自动折叠是**边沿触发**（`splitThink` 返回 closes 计数，仅在未 pin 且设置开时每完成一块折一次）；点 summary 即 **pin** 用户选择（`thinkPinned`，点击 handler 自己 `preventDefault` 持有开合）；DONE 把 pin 带进静态块。**不变量：`thinkEl.open` 没有其他写入者**——未来任何自动开合改动必须过 pin 检查。think 体内部滚动：`.think-body` 高度帽 + `overscroll-behavior: contain`，活块 `thinkStick` 贴底（用户上滚 >24px 即放手），`.think-body pre` 强制 `pre-wrap`（4 空格缩进解析出的代码块会横溢——真机报告第二半）；CSS 被 render-contract-lockstep.test.mjs 钉住；滚动监听必须局部捕获 body 元素（DONE/destroy 时置 null）。
  - **图形文字贴合（2026-10-07，CJK 溢出）**：`fitSvgShapesToText`（getBBox/getCTM 实测；保守规则承重——仅「直接 shape 子恰 1 个且嵌套组无 shape」的父组、只放不缩、增幅 ≤2×、path 跳过、带旋转/斜切的矩阵拒绝；jsdom 无 getBBox 静默跳过）+ `timelineBreakLongRuns`（仅 `timeline` fence，无空格长run 每 ~10 全角字符插**精确** `<br>`——wrap 函数的字面 token，`<br/>` 不匹配；幂等；错误卡/修复按钮永远展示发原始源码）。Tests: lib-render.test.mjs 图形文本贴合块。
  - **图形文字跟正文一致（2026-10-14 用户报告）**：mermaid/echarts/dot/markmap 文字的族·字号跟随面板正文——render.js `panelFontFamily()`/`bubbleFontSize()` 每次渲染现读 computed style（`--msg-font-family`/`--msg-font-size` 变量，`--msg-font-family` 由设置页「正文字体」（storage `fontFamily`，候选表 lib/font-choices.js，'system'=移除内联 var 落回 :root CSS 默认）驱动，改设置新图即跟手）；mermaid 键=`主题|字体` 防漏。**正文字体三档（system/serif/mono）是用户拍板的收窄（2026-10-14），勿再扩**：楷体部分 Linux 缺字形、Inter 等未内置字体列了会「点了没反应」、自定义栈是工程面——要扩先解决跨平台字形保证（如子集化打包）。**旧 mermaid 18px 基准字号补偿就此作废勿复活**（当年为窄面板缩放可读性拍的板；宽度杠杆是 nodeSpacing/rankSpacing 不是字号）。graphviz 只把默认 `Times,serif` 改写为面板栈（模型自选 fontname 保留；字号不动——布局按它量的）；markmap 走 sidepanel.css 覆盖 vendor `--markmap-font` 变量（值引用正文两变量，specificity 高于注入规则，顺序无关）。兜底字面量与 sidepanel.css 正文排版由 render-contract-lockstep.test.mjs 互相钉死，改任一侧必跑。
  - **Mermaid 宿主责任（2026-10-19，pele 评估后的借鉴批）**：**不换渲染器**——pele 是独立实现、无 HTML 标签/KaTeX，会砍掉 `MERMAID_LABEL_MATH_RULE` 教给模型的标签内公式（重评条件见末尾）。借鉴落地四条，勿回退：① `mermaid.initialize` 显式 pin `maxTextSize`(50k)/`maxEdges`(2000，mermaid 默认 500)/`secure` 列表（**mermaid 的 secure 是整表替换默认值**：默认五项含 `suppressErrorRendering` + `themeCSS/themeVariables/fontFamily/altFontFamily/fontSize/dompurifyConfig` 全部列全），SVG 输出 >4M 在落 DOM 前拒绝；**mermaid 12 的 front matter `config:` 绕过 directive 消毒器**（`cleanAndMerge` 直连 merge，`secure` 与 `<`/`>`/`url(data:` 过滤都不经过）——模型可借 `themeCSS` 注入页面级 CSS、抬 `maxTextSize/maxEdges`，`stripFrontMatterConfig` 在进 mermaid 前把整块 `config:` 剥掉（重复 `config:`、引号键、跨行 flow 集合、块标量都覆盖；`title`/`displayMode` 保留；**被剥行用空行占位，行数不变——mermaid 的 parse error 行号必须仍指向原始 source**；per-diagram config 不在我们的 prompt 契约里）；limit/RangeError 经 `mermaidErrorKind` 归类为「图过于复杂」的人话提示（不套 parse-error 格式；修复按钮仍保留，由用户主动发起），其余非 parse 错误按 pele summary 只显示首行+末行。② `securityLevel:'loose'` 只为标签内 KaTeX；**永不调用 mermaid 的 bindFunctions**（loose 会允许模型绑 click 回调）；`sanitizeMermaidSvg` 之后 `enforceSafeDiagramLinks` 再过一道 scheme 白名单（链接 http/https/mailto/tel，图片 http/https；相对/协议相对一律剥，外链统一 `rel`）。③ 方向切换 `findMermaidFlowDirection`/`toggleMermaidDirection`（仅显式 LR/RL/TD/TB/BT，跳过 front matter/`%%`；RL 可还原；只改首行 token）——工具条按钮只在识别出方向的 flowchart 出现。④ 宽度重排观察 **`#messages`** 而非每个 wrapper（气泡宽度随内容变会自激），≥40px、300ms 防抖、只重排视口内的图；重渲事务化（渲染+消毒成功才提交，失败旧图原样保留），SVG 逐字节相同则不碰 DOM。CJK 断行升级为标点感知（收尾标点不到行首、开头标点不吊行尾，最多多带 3 字保证幂等）+ 有 OffscreenCanvas 时按真实字体度量取宽（jsdom 回退 1/0.5，测试确定性）。**Quadrant 整图消失（2026-10-20 用户报告）**：Mermaid 12.2 默认主题的 `quadrantPointFill = ... darken(quadrant1Fill)` 漏传 amount，生成 `hsl(240, 100%, NaN%)`；`sanitizeMermaidSvg` 拒绝任何含 `NaN` 属性的 SVG，于是整张 quadrantChart 被丢弃。`initMermaidTheme` 显式 pin `quadrantPointFill:'#9370DB'`（mermaid 默认 primaryBorderColor 紫）。真机 Chrome 审计 23 种图（flowchart/sequence/class/state/er/journey/gantt/pie/quadrant/requirement/gitGraph/C4/mindmap/timeline/sankey/xychart/block/packet/architecture/radar/treemap/kanban，含用户这张中文图）全部 no-NaN 且过消毒；`zenuml`/`flowchart-elk` 非 core 插件，未装即出错误卡。Tests: `test/lib-mermaid-quadrant.test.mjs` + `lib-render.test.mjs`（方向/白名单/错误分型/标点断行/锚定缩放/front matter）+ `render-contract-lockstep.test.mjs` 第 5–6 节（limits/secure/no-bindFunctions/白名单/重排阈值/事务化/缩放 a11y/字体重排/sequence wrap source pins）+ `locales-parity`（五个新 key 七语同步）。**第二批（同日，pele zoom/mount 借鉴）**：缩放升级为**光标锚定**（`computeAnchoredZoom` 纯函数）、范围 0.2–4（上线当天用户拍板：**静止时也要能缩小**，pele 的 fit 下限不采纳——<1 露出图外留白属预期；平移/方向键/拖拽仍只在 >fit(1) 时启用，`_MM_FIT` 与下限分离）、键盘 `+/−/0/Esc/方向键`（`svgWrap.tabIndex=0` + `mermaidZoomHint` aria-label）、双击放大/复位、Pointer Events 单指平移+双指捏合（元素级 pointer capture；旧的模块级 window 监听已删）、拖动结束落在链接上吞 click、按钮在边界禁用（CSS `.mermaid-btn:disabled`）；**字体/字号设置变更后重排已渲染图**（`refreshMermaidDiagrams`，由 sidepanel 的 `applyFontSize`/`applyFontFamily` 调用；重渲前必须 `initMermaidTheme(state.m)`，否则布局仍按旧字体；**先同步重排视口内的图、屏幕外的排进 `requestIdleCallback`，generation 取消旧批次**）；**输入短路**：`source`/`width`/`themeKey` 都没变的图不再付一次完整渲染（fit-content 气泡在面板变宽时宽度可能不变；重复点同一字号档位 themeKey 不变）；模块级监听 `prefers-color-scheme` 变化并调 `refreshMermaidDiagrams()`——旧 SVG 不会自己换色，主题热切换必须重排（其余渲染器仍只在重渲时换主题，属既有边界）；布局宽度扣 padding（`_contentWidth`，pele `room()`）；**方向档位间距**：`initMermaidTheme(m, variant)` 的 key 含 variant——横向 30/40（省宽）、纵向（TD）50/60（宽度不再是约束，宽松间距防 edge label 挤节点，用户报告「横转纵后排版差」的定向修复）；`sequence: { wrap: true }` 让长消息在窄栏折行；`getMermaidDiagramKind`/`estimateMermaidPreviewHeight` 跳过 front matter；PNG 导出的 raster 尺寸改为**宽高双向封顶**（只限宽时，100×200000 的图会生成 200×400000 的 canvas → OOM）。**图标包（`fa:fa-*`）评估后未接**：mermaid 需要 `registerIconPacks` 的 Iconify 形状，jsdom 缺 getBBox 无法端到端验证，不值得盲上。**pele 重评条件**：它提供受信 math/HTML-label 通道，或我们主动放弃 mermaid 标签内公式，或稳定数月并经 Obsidian 大版本验证——三者任一满足再重开「换渲染器」议题。
  - **净化三坑（勿回退）**：mermaid 输出先过 `sanitizeMermaidSvg()`（`securityLevel:'loose'` 是节点标签里 KaTeX 公式所需，代价是放行 foreignObject）；DOMPurify 的 `ALLOWED_URI_REGEXP` **不管** `img/video/audio` 的 `data:` URI（自家 `DATA_URI_TAGS` 清单绕开该正则）——拦 `data:image/svg+xml` 用模块加载时注册一次的 `uponSanitizeAttribute` hook；**绝不把 `ALLOWED_URI_REGEXP` 直接当 sanitize 选项传**——它会作用于所有非内部安全清单属性**的值**，严格白名单会静默剥掉 `<ol start="N">`/`<td colspan>`/checkbox（真 bug：编号列表第二块从「2.」变「1.」）；URI 校验只挂在 `URI_ATTRS` 集合上。
  - **用户气泡受限渲染（2026-09-26）**：`renderUserContent` 只渲染围栏+行内 code、其余 verbatim 文本——刻意不做全量 Markdown（用户输入的 `#`/URL 常无 Markdown 意图）；`dataset.raw` 永远是逐字原文；裸围栏走 `hljs.highlightAuto`（relevance>5；`_HLJS_AUTO_SUBSET` 单份勿复制；顺带修的两个预存 hljs bug 见 wiki）。Tests: lib-render.test.mjs。
- `reveal-pacer.js` — `createRevealPacer(onReveal)`, a thin wrapper around the vendored `markstream-core` package's `createSmoothMarkdownStream`. Paces how fast enqueued deltas become visible (self-terminating internal `requestAnimationFrame` loop, confirmed via source read — no leak risk even if `destroy()` is skipped, though calling it is still correct hygiene). Used by `render.js`'s `makeStreamRenderer` (which is now also the detail-thread card's renderer — the card's old private accumulate loop was removed so live thinking/pacing/teardown can never drift from the main chat).
- `turn-chrome.js` (2026-09-23 C8) — the single owner of the per-turn chrome shared by the main chat and every detail-thread card: the 思考中… Ns wait indicator, the pre-bubble `.tool-progress` line, the folded `.tool-history` details, the de-duplicating token-usage chip, and the approval/clarification cards. `createTurnChrome({key, relay, scope})` per surface — `key` pins the relay map key at render time (`tabId` re-read at click time would mis-target after a tab switch), `relay` carries the per-surface message names (APPROVAL_RESPOND vs SUBCHAT_APPROVAL_RESPOND), `scope` pins card lookups so coexisting surfaces never collide. `addProviderChip` (the sender-label stamp) is also exported from here — sidepanel.js's byte-identical private `addProviderLabel` twin was deleted 2026-09-30 (批D). Converged drifts it now owns: the reason-carrying approval-failure toast. **Usage chip 的 t/s 只对逐回合通道生效（2026-10-20 用户报告 Hermes 1485 t/s）**：Hermes 的 `run.completed.usage` 是**会话累计**（`session_*` 计数器），除以本地 first-chunk→DONE 计时器会得到荒谬速率。规则：逐回合通道（OpenAI/DeepSeek/Responses/Anthropic/opencode/bridge）显示 `t/s = 本回合 completion ÷ 生成窗口`；累计通道（Hermes/OpenSquilla）**不显示累计值，也不空白**：Hermes 的 `/v1/runs` 有两种部署形态——原生路径（`_run_usage(agent)`）返回会话累计且**必带** `cache_read_tokens`/`cache_write_tokens`；session-authority 路径（`run_turn_runner`）存的已是逐轮差值，只有 `input/output/total`。`lib/usage-snapshots.js` 的 `takeTurnUsage` 用「有没有 cache 键」判别：有 → 持久化基线做差值；没有 → 原样返回、绝不二次相减。升级后第一次遇到无基线的老会话先隐藏一轮再建立基线；计数器变小（会话重建）整份快照作为本轮。差值失败/无基线时宁可不显示 chip，绝不回退为累计总量（`usageChipModel` 的 `scope:'session'` 仍是安全网）。OpenSquilla 在 `squillaStream` 里取 `usage.status` 前后快照做差值。**UI 不出现 session/turn 标签**（用户 2026-10-20 明确要求不做这种区分）。耗时是客户端实测的「首 token → DONE」窗口，tooltip 明确写出；不要把它伪装成 provider 数据。Tests: `test/lib-usage-snapshots.test.mjs`。**输入框不显示任何计数（2026-10-21 用户拍板）**：先删了 `~N tokens` 客户端启发式（CJK 1 字/token、非 CJK 4 字符/token 会误导），随后连精确字符数也删了——输入框下方的 `#composerinfo` 现在只承载「+N images」附图计数；token 数只在回合结束后由 provider usage 给出（回复下的 chip）。**Usage 归一化单点 `lib/usage.js`（2026-10-20）**：`normalizeUsage` 统一 OpenAI `prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens` / DeepSeek `prompt_cache_hit_tokens` / Responses `input_tokens_details.cached_tokens` / Anthropic `cache_read_input_tokens` / Hermes `cache_read_tokens` / 桥的顶层 `cached_tokens`（Anthropic/Hermes 的 `input_tokens` 不含 cache 读写，prompt 总数要把 cache read/write 加回；有 `prompt_tokens` 时按 inclusive 处理）；chip 在通道上报缓存字段时显示 `cache NN.N%`（保留 1 位小数，99.6% 不再圆整成「全命中」），tooltip 带 cached/cache-write 绝对值，无缓存字段的通道不显示假 0%。续写两腿的 usage 合并走 `mergeUsage`（递归累加嵌套 details）——旧内联合并只加顶层数字，缓存计数会丢。Tests: `test/lib-usage.test.mjs`。
- `outline-rail.js` (2026-08-31, user-requested cherry-studio MessageAnchorLine port) — the conversation outline rail on #messages' right edge: per-turn ticks (user message + its replies = one turn), click-to-scroll + flash, scroll-follow highlight, hover preview of the turn's user message. Self-heals across renderHistory wipes (MutationObserver rebuild; events MUST be bound on each rebuilt strip, not once at init — two shipped incidents pinned in its header comment).
- `image-drop.js` (2026-09-30 批D) — the shared image-attach pipeline for BOTH image surfaces (main composer + detail-thread cards): `fileToDataUrl`, the 20MB pre-read gate, the `imageRejectReason` budget gate + rejection copy (`imageNotAttachedText`), `ingestImageFiles(fileList, store, notify)`, `pastedImageFiles(e)` and the `.imagepreview` strip builder (`renderImageStrip`). The per-surface notify style (compact appendError vs showToast) is the ONLY sanctioned difference — the two private copies had already drifted once. Leaf module: imports only i18n + image-budget.
- **KaTeX Worker 与数学提取守卫** — 裁决（勿移除）：`renderSafe` 的块级/行内 `$…$` 提取正则在任何数学分支之前逐字消费 `\` 与 `\$`，否则 `\$8K` 这类转义美元会被配对成公式；行内 `$` 采用 **Pandoc 的定界规则**（开闭紧贴、闭合符后不接数字），拒绝的候选原样返回；`fixBoldSpans` 的字符类用 `\p{P}∪\p{S}`（`\p{P}` 会漏 `$`/`×`）且邻接判定接受任意 `\p{L}∪\p{N}`。**两处字符类都不得收窄**（810 例 fuzz 从 102 错降到 0）。worker 批量渲染整条消息的公式，失败整条回落同步，绝不 worker/sync 混用。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **Rendering-Pipeline-zh「KaTeX Worker 与数学提取守卫」**
- `mermaid-utils.js` — `estimateMermaidPreviewHeight`/`clampMermaidPreviewHeight` (sets the raw code-fence placeholder's height before the async render starts, reducing the jump when it's swapped for the real diagram) and `escapeSequenceTextSemicolons` (works around a real mermaid.js parser bug: a bare `;` inside sequence-diagram message/Note text — e.g. dialogue quoting SQL — breaks the parser; escaped to `#59;` on retry via `renderMermaidWithRetry`, which never touches semicolons that legitimately start a new mermaid statement).
- `history-reconcile.js` — `planHistoryReconcile({ entries, nextHistoryIdx, anchorH, anchorRaw })`: pure decision for what a `nextHistoryIdx > storage.length` drift means. The two causes need OPPOSITE responses — auto-trim removed entries from the FRONT (shift every bubble's hidx down) vs a tail append that never happened (provider threw before storing the user turn; just reset the counter). Distinguished by anchoring on the lowest-hidx visible bubble: its exact raw content still at its own storage index → counter-only drift; at `index − drift` → real trim. Undecidable → reset-only, which is safe now that delete paths verify `res.data.ok` instead of the bridge envelope's always-true `res.ok` (the 0.33.0 sweep: single/multiselect delete, UNDO_ATTACH, TRUNCATE, ATTACH_*_CONFIRM, APPROVAL/CLARIFY all read the inner payload; STREAM_PEEK reads `res.data`; resume/stream-error paths now clean up `activeController` + port). Introduced as a module because the decision is unit-testable without the full sidepanel DOM harness.
- **字幕抽屉（transcript-drawer.js）** — 裁决：**刻意不移植同类产品的学习型签名功能**（双语对照字幕、逐行词汇/笔记捕获）——browsa 不是语言学习工具；技术手法可借鉴（如 youtube-digest 的章节覆盖提示成了 `coverageHintEn/Zh`），**对端产品的可见签名功能不借**。抽屉开启时轮询播放位置，tick 有 seq guard（慢响应不得覆盖新位置），连续 4 次失败安静收摊。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **Architecture-zh「字幕抽屉 / 视频时间线」**
- `sessions-ui.js` — sessions drawer (list/pin/rename/export/delete/load): GET_SESSIONS takes a `q` that the background filters against names AND message content (`lib/storage.js`'s `getSavedSessions(q)`, which also sorts pinned first and flags content-only hits — the drawer renders a `.session-content-hit` dot for those); pinned rows (`PIN_SESSION`) lose their delete button entirely; unpinned rows use a two-step armed delete (first click arms `.armed` for 2s, second click commits, Ctrl/Cmd+click skips) instead of a confirm dialog; the unfiltered list groups under Pinned + Today/Yesterday/This week/Earlier buckets (local-midnight boundaries). `loadSession` needs `initSessionsUI({ cancelActiveStream, renderHistory, scrollToBottom, clearPendingImages })`. **Session-organization philosophy (two explicit user rejections, 2026-09-05 — do not re-propose)**: (1) a tab-scoped-sessions feature (per-tab conversation swap behind a topbar toggle) was built and removed — auto-switching the conversation on tab switches took control away from the user, who prefers explicit organization (new topic = new session via the drawer's button); the global single conversation is the intended default. (2) a per-message "context receipt" (collapsible 本次发送了什么 disclosing what a turn sent — attachments/chars/images/resent history) was built and removed as not useful; the attach chip's「检查」/「撤销」buttons remain the only sent-content inspection UI. Both were fully deleted from the codebase, not just disabled.
- `followups.js` — queued follow-up messages: `onSend()` called while a stream is active enqueues into the dock above the composer instead of starting a second parallel CHAT (two ports on one tabId used to fight over the same chunk feed and corrupt history indices — a real latent bug this closes). `maybeDrainFollowups()` (sidepanel.js) auto-sends the oldest one after DONE, guarded against clobbering a non-empty composer (retry/edit flows park text there); user-canceled streams never reach DONE so Esc keeps the queue. `initFollowups({ mountEl, sendNow })`; clicking a chip sends it immediately.
- `composer-state.js` — per-panel draft persistence (debounced `chrome.storage.local` under `composerState`: `{ draft, history }`) so unsent text survives the side panel being torn down, plus ↑/↓ input-history recall. **One recall SCOPE per input surface** (`createInputHistory({storageKey, persistPayload})`, 2026-09-21): the module-level exports are the MAIN composer's scope; detail-thread cards create their own (`subchatInputHistory`, `{history}` only — no draft) — the user explicitly directed that ↑ in a 追问 card recalls follow-up questions ONLY, never main-composer sends, so do NOT merge the scopes back into one list. Recall mechanics (all scopes): eligible only when the input is empty/all-selected/caret-at-boundary, vetoable by the caller (slash panel open), IME-safe (`isComposing`), ↓ past the newest entry restores the pre-navigation draft verbatim; typing/deleting/pasting ends recall WITHOUT replacing the edited value (`resetHistoryNav(input, {restoreDraft:false})`), while explicit send/clear resets keep the default draft restore before clearing. Never use a restoring reset in an input-event listener: it discards the edit (2026-10-01 audit regression). Consecutive dupes collapse, 20 entries. **In-progress walk state is PER-INPUT within a scope** (a `WeakMap` keyed by the input element, holding `{idx, savedDraft}`) — an armed walk must never leak its position or pre-nav draft into another input, and a closed card's stale walk dies with its detached element. Only the main composer uses `attachDraftPersistence`/`restoreComposerState`.
- `error-classifier.js` — `classifyErrorText(raw)` maps provider/network error strings to i18n headline keys (auth/429/model/timeout/network/5xx, first-match-wins); null for unmatched text so `appendError()` keeps short instructional notices as the old compact single-line chip and only builds the full error card (classified headline + 3-line-clamped raw + collapsible copyable raw) when there's something real to explain.
- `multiselect.js` — bulk-delete mode; `deleteSelectedMessages` needs `initMultiselect({ decrementNextHistoryIdx })` to keep `sidepanel.js`'s history-index counter in sync.
- `msg-search.js` — Ctrl+F in-conversation search. Fully self-contained.
- `math-copy.js` (2026-09-18) — 选中复制含 KaTeX 公式的选区时，剪贴板里是 LaTeX 源码（`$…$`/`$$…$$`）而不是 MathML 碎片。根因：公式以 `output:'mathml'` 渲染，浏览器把 `<math>` 序列化成纯文本时逐记号插换行（`"Attention\n(\nQ\n,..."`），用户报告「公式没法拷贝到其他文档」；LaTeX 源码其实一直在 KaTeX 自带的 `<annotation encoding="application/x-tex">` 里。`initMathCopy({ messagesEl })` 在 `init()` 装一个 document 级 `copy` 监听：**只有选区真的碰到 `.katex` 才接管**（其余复制保持浏览器原生行为一字不改，包括 composer/textarea 的 `_fallbackCopy`）；选区只盖到公式一部分（在公式内部起止拖拽）时按整个公式吸附（`intersectsNode` + 边界扩到 `.katex` 全身）；克隆选区 → `<math>` 换成 LaTeX 文本节点 → 剥掉面板交互件（`.msg-actions`/`.code-copy-btn`/`.msg-time`/`.token-usage`/`.tool-history`/各 `summary`，注意**只剥面板自己的类**，模型自产的 `<details><summary>` 保留）→ 离屏容器（`position:fixed;left:-99999px`，**不能 display:none，innerText 会退化成 textContent**）读 `innerText` 得到保留段落/表格/代码块结构的文本，`text/plain` 与 `text/html` 都写同源内容后 `preventDefault`（不 preventDefault 时 Chrome 会用原生序列化覆盖 setData——已实测）。`.katex-error`（无 `<math>`）不处理，其文本本就是源码。整条链路 try/catch fail-open：拦截出错绝不能弄坏普通复制。**悬浮复制按钮**（同日，用户明确要 mermaid 式一键复制）：`addMathCopyButtons(root)` 在三处渲染点与 `addCodeCopyButtons` 并排调用（sidepanel DONE、renderHistory 异步升级、detail-thread postRender），给每个 `.katex`（含行内）挂 `.mermaid-toolbar.math-toolbar` + 单个 `.mermaid-btn`（title 走新 i18n key `mathCopyLatex`，en/zh 已同步），点击时**重读** annotation（流式重渲染会整体换 DOM，不信任闭包旧值）经 `_copyText` 写 `formatTex` 结果并 ✓ 反馈；CSS：`.katex{position:relative}`，行内按钮 `top:-30px` 浮在公式上方（base 的 `top:6px` 会盖住符号），块级用 `:has(> math[display="block"])` 落回 mermaid 式角落，hover 显现规则并入既有 `.mermaid-toolbar` 那条；`.mermaid-toolbar` 本就在复制剥离清单里，选区复制不会带出按钮。
- `detail-thread.js` — the "select text → 细聊/追问" side-conversation card. Fully self-contained; wires its own `mouseup`/`scroll` listeners on import. Rendering goes through the MAIN chat's `makeStreamRenderer`（活 thinking 折叠、paced reveal、`.destroy()` 中途干净拆除——推理内容本就随 delta 到达，缺的只是 UI）。裁决（完整机制叙事见 wiki Architecture「Detail thread」）：
  - **停止键**：流中 send 键换 ■（`.is-stopping`，与主输入同款）；点击发 `SUBCHAT_ABORT` 且**保留卡片**——有部分文本就地收尾（`subMessages` 保持 user/assistant 交替可继续追问），零文本则删空气泡并弹回未答的用户轮（fail() 语义）。
  - **回复铭牌**：`SUBCHAT_DONE` 带 `providerLabel`/`providerKey`——subchat-handler 必须用与 CHAT 同一对 `providerEntryLabel`+`resolveReplyModelOrEndpoint`，**禁止按 `isHermes` 分支**——`test/subchat.test.mjs` 禁该 token 且正则锁死 `pushSubChatChunk(… SUBCHAT_DONE …)` 源码行，改行必改正则。chip 前置气泡首位。
  - **滚动解耦**：`.detail-thread-messages` 设 `overscroll-behavior: contain`（嵌套滚动器边界不再链滚）。
  - **Agent 过程面**：三个 agent 分支推 `SUBCHAT_TOOL_PROGRESS`/`SUBCHAT_APPROVAL`/`SUBCHAT_CLARIFY`；卡内同一套 chrome 全部收敛在 `turn-chrome.js`+`tool-tier.js`（无卡内副本），卡只按 `data-sub-id` pin；回复经 `SUBCHAT_APPROVAL_RESPOND`/`SUBCHAT_CLARIFY_RESPOND` 孪生消息（pending 条目在 subchat-handler 自己的 subId Map）。finalize/stop/fail 跑 `clearTurnChrome(el)`。等待指示器同槽（思考中… Ns，agent 每请求新建 agent、首 delta 前曾全程沉默）。回合收尾对齐：`toolEvents` 折成同一款 tool-history（中止丢弃、仅 DONE 折叠），`SUBCHAT_DONE.usage` 渲染同一款 token chip（OpenAI 与 runs 两套字段都吃）。
  - **↑/↓ 召回自有 scope（2026-09-21 用户拍板，ADR-0006）**：`createInputHistory({storageKey:'subchatInputHistory'})` 模块级共享所有卡——**与主输入刻意分离**：卡内 ↑ 只召回追问，绝不召回主聊天发送。`send()` 先 `pushInputHistory(q)` 再 `resetHistoryNav(input)` **后才清空**（顺序反了会把 pre-nav 草稿写回已清空的输入）；草稿现在随卡片一起持久化（下一颗），召回表仍是独立的 storage key/scope。
  - **图片（2026-09-25）**：摄取走共享 `lib/sidepanel/image-drop.js`；图随 SUBCHAT `images` 字段——LLM 走同一批 message-builder（各方言多模态形状，纯文本输出逐字节不变）、opencode/bridge 直传、**squilla 刻意丢弃**（网关合同不收粘贴图，与主聊天同）。
  - **刻意不与 CHAT 合并**：无 `appendToHistory`、per-subId agent 会话、上下文只限引用+问题——隔离是追问的产品承诺，复用只发生在 builder/stream/chrome 层。
  - **引文高亮与折叠（2026-10-21 用户要求）**：选区原文用 **CSS Custom Highlight API**（注册名 `detail-thread-quote`，CSS 在 sidepanel.css）在宿主回复里持续高亮；Range 不写 DOM，靠记录里的文本偏移 `thread.highlightRange` 在 `runUpgrade`、主聊天 DONE、错误重渲、面板恢复之后由 `refreshDetailHighlights(bubbleEl)` 重建（偏移因 provider 铭牌/时间戳等 chrome 文本差异漂移时，回退到按 `quotedText` 在渲染文本里查找；`::highlight` 底色走 `--gold`，页边批注语义）。卡片头部折叠按钮（`ICONS.chevron`，`.collapsed` 隐藏 messages/imgs/输入行/缩放手柄、保留引用；折叠态与高度持久化）；**侧栏重开（新文档、`activeCards` 为空）时恢复的卡一律自动折叠**（`autoCollapse: freshPanel`），同一文档内的 `renderHistory` 恢复保持卡片当前开合状态；X 仍是关闭并中止在途回合并删记录。**任何替换气泡 innerHTML 的路径都必须先 `captureDetailCardsIn(el)`、渲染后 `reinsertDetailCards(el, captured)`**（主 DONE/错误恢复/历史 `runUpgrade` 已接；`moveSiblingDetailCards(el)` 负责把同步渲染缺块时暂落后气泡的卡移回锚点）。Tests: `lib-detail-thread-persist`（折叠持久化 + 高亮 Range 恢复/关闭即移除）。
  - **跨侧栏关闭的持久化（2026-10-20 用户报告「关掉 side panel 再打开追问就没了」）**：卡片状态（引用、显示消息、wire 转录、草稿、高度、宿主指纹、锚点）经 `lib/sidepanel/detail-thread-store.js` 存进 **`chrome.storage.session`**（key `browsa.detailThreads.v1`，按 subId 一条、上限 12、写入串行化、失败 fail-open）。`restoreDetailThreads()` 在 `renderHistory()` 同步渲染后按 `hidx` + 宿主 raw 的 FNV 指纹重找气泡（流式生长用 `hostPrefix` 兜底），再按 `anchorPath`（子节点索引链）重找段落/`li` 锚点；宿主找不到时卡片留在消息流末尾，**不许静默丢**。**`renderHistory` 的异步 `renderSafe` 升级会 `el.innerHTML` 重建气泡**——`runUpgrade` 必须把嵌套卡按锚点路径重插（`anchorPathFrom`/`resolveAnchorPath`），并处理「同步渲染缺块时暂落后气泡的卡」：升级完成后按 `card.dataset.anchorPath` 把它移回真正的 `li`/段落。面板卸载：`pagehide` / `visibilitychange(hidden)` / `unload` 都 flush（**Chrome 关侧栏不保证 pagehide**），pagehide 再停端口/SW_PING/pacer；流式文本**首块立即落盘、其后 400ms 节流**（旧 1s 防抖在关面板时会让整段已到文本丢失，真机复现）；恢复出的 `inFlight` 回合按 `stopTurn` 语义收尾（保留已流式文本 + 发 `SUBCHAT_ABORT`）。**删记录的唯一入口**：关闭卡片、删宿主消息/重新生成/编辑重发/多选删除（`closeDetailThreadsIn`）、新会话/清空/切会话（`clearDetailThreads`）。图 data URL 刻意不落盘（session 配额 10MB，一张卡最多 8MB 图，写爆会连累整个 key）。**不要改用 `chrome.storage.local`、不要写进主 history/`savedSessions`**：它不是主历史，只活在当前浏览器会话。Tests: `test/lib-detail-thread-persist.test.mjs`（含首块立即落盘、宿主删除联动、重复恢复）。
  - **jsdom 测试坑**：结束一回合而不发 DONE/停止/关卡，会泄漏回合端口的 20s `SW_PING` setInterval 挂死 `node --test`——每个 detail-thread 测试必须拆回合。
  - **引用公式（2026-09-26 用户报告）**：选区抓取用 math-copy.js 的 `selectionTextWithMath(sel)`——公式吸附为整体写成 LaTeX（`sel.toString()` 对公式只拿到 MathML 逐记号换行碎片；这条文本同时是引用显示源和 SUBCHAT 模型上下文）；引用块挂载后跑 `renderMathInPlainText`（刻意不走 renderSafe 全量 Markdown；delimiters 只认自家产物，落单 `$` 不动）；与复制路径共享 `mathifiedSelectionFragment` 内核且对 **克隆** 做 expand（直接 expand 活选区会让用户可见选区跳变）。
- `icons.js` / `ui-utils.js` — leaf modules with no dependencies on the others: the `ICONS` SVG map, and `$`/`escM`/`_copyText`/`showToast`/`showConfirmDialog`/`sendMessage`/`_findCard`/`_insertCard`/`isImeComposing`. **`isImeComposing(e)` is the ONE IME guard for every Enter-submits gate** (`e.isComposing === true || e.keyCode === 229`, 2026-09-21): a bare `!e.isComposing` misses IME drivers that report only the 229 process-key code, and a missing guard outright sends half-typed text — the 追问 card's send Enter shipped without any guard (user-reported: IME-confirm Enter fired the unfinished question). Every new input that submits on Enter must read `!isImeComposing(e)`; self-contained modules that must not import ui-utils (composer-state's `handleHistoryNav`) inline the same two-signal check. Deliberately unguarded: the screenshot-crop canvas Enter (no text input exists) and overlay-close convenience Enters.

### Chemistry / biostructure rendering (`renderSmiles` / `renderPdb`, 2026-09-13)

Two more instances of the fenced-block→live-render pattern, for formats LLMs natively speak:

- **```smiles → RDKit** — 裁决：RDKit 的 `get_mol`/`get_rxn` 返回 NULL 即**校验失败**，插 `.smiles-error` 提示且原块保留（绝不静默画错）；含 `>` 走 `get_rxn`（普通分子 SMILES 不可能含 `>`，判别无损）。**CPK 杂原子配色是化学语义，绝不用 CSS 反转 SVG**（反转会把氧变青）。`parsed.delete()` 必须在 finally 释放 WASM 堆。**RDKit glue 的 CSP 补丁在 build 时做，锚点失配即构建失败**；`__emval_get_method_caller` 的 eval 站点刻意不打（MinimalLib 不可达，且失败 fail-open）。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **Rendering-Pipeline-zh「SMILES → RDKit 渲染」**
- **` ```pdb `** → `parsePdbBlock`（纯函数，导出供测试）分类：裸 4 字符 ID（`1UBQ`）→ fetch `https://files.rcsb.org/download/<ID>.pdb` 渲染；**AlphaFold 预测模型**（`AF-{UniProt acc}-F{n}` / 全文件名 / 裸 UniProt 登录号 6+ 字符不与 4 字符 RCSB ID 冲突）→ 经 DB API 解析**当前**模型文件（版本会前进，P00533 已 v4→v6；硬编码版本 URL 必 404；优先精确 `modelEntityId`、退化 `entries[0]`）；内容匹配 ATOM 系 → 内联渲染；其余（含登录号不合语法的 `AF-`）→ 留作文本。**渲染用 Mol*（2026-09-29 换掉 3Dmol.js，用户拍板「业内都用 Mol*」）**：`getMolstar()` 懒加载 RAW 拷贝的 IIFE + css；**`layoutShowControls` 连 TOP 区一起门控**——关掉它序列条就死，保持开、逐个隐藏其他区。构建链 `data.rawData → parseTrajectory → hierarchy.applyPreset`：**applyPreset 的字符串查找只认 hierarchy 预设，representation id 传那里会静默 no-op**（真机发现：状态树停在 trajectory 无报错）——必须走 `representationPreset`。AF 模型用内置 plddt 预设（调色板与 AF DB 四带逐字节一致，自家 `plddtColor` 已删、molstar 持有；`.pdb-legend` 保留为可见图例）；普通结构用 auto 预设。一切 fetch/preset 失败 fail-open：还原原始代码块 + `dispose()` 掉已建 viewer（WebGL 上下文）。**模型永不吐原始 ATOM 坐标**（CAPABILITY_HINTS 与 AGENT_RENDER_HINT 双镜像钉住，只认 ID、永不编造）。CORS：RCSB 不发 ACAO 但 `<all_urls>` host_permissions 直接过；AF 两端都发 `*`。PNG 导出读 `canvas.toDataURL()`（连续渲染，实测能截到帧）；所有活 viewer 进模块级 Set，`disposeMolstarViewers()` 在 DOM 擦除前调用（浏览器 ~16 个 WebGL 上下文上限）；拖拽调宽走容器 RO → `plugin.canvas3d.requestResize()`（molstar 只跟窗口不跟容器；RO 挂 `_chartObservers`）。**纯围栏回复拿全宽气泡**（`.msg.assistant:has(...)` 加宽规则——fit-content 会把百分比尺寸的 viewer 压成细条，六个活渲染 wrapper 一条规则组全覆盖；render-contract-lockstep.test.mjs 钉住）。Tests: `test/lib-chem-render.test.mjs`（`window.molstar` stub 精确镜像 renderPdb 触碰的面）。CSP 补丁与「必须真机验证」教训见 Rendering-Pipeline wiki；换型叙事（含 applyPreset 静默 no-op 的排查）同页。
- **Mol*'s dist must NEVER go through esbuild** (RAW-copied in build.mjs from `node_modules/molstar/build/viewer`, loaded by `getMolstar()` via a classic `<script>` tag + `<link>` for the css): the dist is a prebuilt IIFE assigning `window.molstar` — re-bundling 5.2MB of already-built code buys nothing and risks breaking its internal webpack runtime. The bundle contains ZERO worker spawns.
- **MV3 CSP patch (2026-09-30 field report, VERIFICATION-ENVIRONMENT LESSON)**: the extension page CSP (`script-src 'self' 'wasm-unsafe-eval'`) blocks `new Function`, and the h264-mp4-encoder module (snapshot VIDEO export — dormant for browsa) initializes EAGERLY at bundle evaluation, so embind's eval-based class factories threw EvalErrors that (before the patch) killed the whole IIFE — `window.molstar` never existed and every ```pdb block failed on the real extension. **The original "live run with zero CSP violations" claim was WRONG — it ran in dev-preview, a plain-http page whose default CSP allows eval; only a run inside the real extension (`launchPersistentContext --load-extension`, xvfb) exercises the extension CSP.** build.mjs's copyRaw now patches the vendor on copy: (1) embind `createNamedFunction` eval'd body → computed-property named function; (2) dynCall wrapper factory → rest-args closure (dormant, defensive); (3) the eager h264 init is made LAZY (root fix — no init → no embind registration → the third eval site in craftInvokerFunction never fires, and MV3's `fetch(data:…)` wasm-load block never triggers either). All patches regex-anchor on stable string literals and THROW THE BUILD if a pattern stops matching after a molstar upgrade. Same field report: RDKit's glue needed the same treatment (see the ```smiles bullet). The REAL-extension verification (`/tmp` scripts pattern: seed `chrome.storage.local.history` with fences from options.html, open sidepanel.html in a tab, assert canvas+svg+zero-CSP console) is now the required gate for any vendor swap.
- Wiring: the two renderers are called alongside `renderMermaid`/`renderEcharts`/`renderMarkmap` at all three sites (sidepanel.js DONE + renderHistory async upgrade + detail-thread postRender) and are preloaded by `preloadChartVendors()`. Tests: `test/lib-chem-render.test.mjs` (jsdom has no canvas/WebGL — the tests cover parsePdbBlock, the vendor loaders, the fence→wrapper swap, RCSB/AF fetch shapes, the builder/preset calls via a `window.molstar` stub, and failure-path restoration of the raw block; `getMolstar()`'s module-level promise cache means the stub is installed once per test file, mirroring the pdf worker-client singleton gotcha).
- **Chem toolbar visibility fix (2026-09-14)**: `.smiles-block`/`.pdb-viewer` were never in the `.mermaid-toolbar` hover-reveal rule, so their toolbars sat at `opacity: 0` permanently (still clickable if you guessed the coordinates — invisible buttons). Also `.smiles-block` lacked `position: relative`, so its absolute toolbar anchored to `.msg` (bubble top-right) instead of the diagram. Fixed by adding both to the hover rule + positioning the block.

### Graphviz DOT rendering (` ```dot `, `renderDot`, 2026-09-28 — replaces ```nn)

Fifth instance of the fenced-block→live-render pattern, and the PRIMARY architecture-figure path. **User decision 2026-09-28, do not re-propose a hand-rolled NN-spec renderer**: ````nn` (2026-09-14, deleted in this pass) taught models a custom JSON layer-stack spec, but a format we invented has ZERO model training prior — the model guesses field shapes, and when the tolerant parser drops them the figure silently degrades ("AI 对于 NN 好像没有那么适配"). DOT is the opposite: it is what torchview/torchviz/PyTorch export, models know it cold, and viz-js covers dependency graphs / dataflow / state machines as a bonus. Mermaid keeps flowcharts/sequence/timelines; ````dot` owns blocks-and-arrows figures including every NN architecture. **(2026-10-20 更新，用户问「mermaid 类型只用了一小部分？」后拍板扩菜单)**：Mermaid 类型菜单扩至 state/class/er/journey/gantt/gitGraph/quadrant/requirement/sankey-beta/kanban/timeline——原分流不变（数据图表→echarts、思维导图→markmap、方块箭头/NN→dot），并加窄侧栏条款（优先 TD；gantt/gitGraph/journey/sankey 是宽图）与「禁 %%{init}%%/front-matter config」（后者本就被 stripFrontMatterConfig 剥掉）。**刻意不教的类型**：pie/xychart（echarts 管数据图表）、mindmap（markmap 管）、C4Context/packet/radar/treemap/block-beta（实验性/宽图/窄侧栏收益差）。改动落在 prompt-assembly 的 CAPABILITY_HINTS_ENTRIES 单一文本源，两通道自动同步，增量 ≈1.1K chars。**同日回滚（用户报告「只有要求画图才会画图」+ 偶尔整段纯文本）**：先上线的两段式（ALWAYS ≈1.6K + RENDER 按家族条件追加）与「何时不画」负向规则**同日撤销**——普通问答丢掉 6.3K 契约里的画图指路后行为明显回退，负向规则又雪上加霜。裁决：`CAPABILITY_HINTS_ENTRIES` **每轮全量下发**（CHAT/SUBCHAT/agent 全通道），删除 `CAPABILITY_HINTS_ALWAYS`/`RENDER_CONTRACT_HINTS`/`RENDER_HINT_GROUPS`/`buildRenderHints` 与全部触发词；`MERMAID_TYPE_MENU_RULE` 里的 `Err on the side of NOT drawing … at most one diagram per reply` 整句删除。同时新增两条正向契约：`RICH_MARKDOWN_RULE`（默认富 Markdown——标题/加粗/inline code/列表/表格/引用/带语言标签的代码块；短回答可保持纯文本，但超过两三句就必须有可见结构）与 `FIGURE_PROACTIVITY_RULE`（内容本身有结构——工作原理、架构/流水线、请求响应流、状态机、层级、时间线、二维对比、依赖/数据流——就主动配图，不必等用户点名；一张好图胜过几张冗余图，简单回答不必硬配）。/prompt 检视器恢复为只列 `Capability hints` 一整块（所见即所发，不再重算家族）。**勿再提按关键词/家族门控渲染契约，也勿再加「少画/少排版」负向指令**——省下的 token 远不值行为回退。回归：test/prompt-assembly.test.mjs（全量/无负向规则/富 Markdown/source pin）+ chat-rebuild-channels 的「casual turn carries the full contract」与「EVERY LLM channel」用例 + subchat 的 sysPrompt pin。

- **Vendor**: `@viz-js/viz` (Graphviz compiled to WASM, MIT, actively released — 3.30.0 2026-09). The dist JS carries the `.wasm` base64-INLINE in one self-contained file (`lib/vendor/viz.bundle.js`, ~1.5MB, esbuild-bundled via `VENDORS` entry `viz`; the npm package lives in devDependencies since only the build consumes it). `WebAssembly.instantiate` is covered by the already-present `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` CSP. Lazy-loaded via `getViz()`/`getVizInstance()` (instance cached after first `mod.instance()`); preloaded by `preloadChartVendors()` like the other diagram vendors (fence-gated). **Node/jsdom can REALLY execute it** — `test/lib-dot-render.test.mjs` runs true renders, only needing `globalThis.DOMParser` globalized before importing render.js (`viz.renderSVGElement` builds the SVG through it).
- **`renderDot`**: `code.language-dot` → `viz.renderSVGElement(source)` → `_sanitizeDotSvg` → `rethemeDotSvg(svgEl, isDark)` (Graphviz never emits script/foreignObject/event handlers, but DOT `href`/`URL` attributes become `<a xlink:href=…>` — strip `javascript:` targets and `on*` attrs; the `<a>` shell stays, inert without its href) → wrapper `.dot-diagram` reusing `.mermaid-svg-wrap` styles AND mermaid's `_mermaidToolbar`/`_mermaidInteractions` (viewBox zoom/drag/copy/export are generic viewBox operations). Invalid DOT: error note (`.dot-error`) inserted BEFORE the block, raw code kept (readable, copyable, re-askable) — unlike mermaid there is no AI-repair card (REPAIR_MERMAID is mermaid-parser-specific); revisit only if field reports demand it. **`rethemeDotSvg` (2026-09-30, pure, exported for tests)**: viz-js emits default-black ink over a white canvas polygon — the white canvas is dropped both themes, dark mode remaps DEFAULT-black text/strokes/arrowheads to light (model-chosen non-black colors kept — same principle as `rethemeRdkitSvg`), and a contrast rescue flips node labels to light ink when the node's fill is darker than ~0.35 luminance (field report: model emitted `style=filled, fillcolor=black` without fontcolor — black-on-black boxes; the hint now also bans filling without a contrasting fontcolor). The wrapper is CSS `resize`-able like pdb/smiles/echarts.
- **Prompt side**: `FENCE_INDEX` (prompt-assembly.js) says ````dot (neural nets & graphs)`; the former ```nn CAPABILITY_HINTS entry is now the dot entry — routing rule "Mermaid for flowcharts/sequence/timelines, ```dot whenever the figure is blocks-and-arrows — including every neural-network architecture", teaching `rankdir`/`rank=same`/`cluster`/edge labels with a full one-line example. Both channels inherit via the shared entries automatically. **2026-09-30 field report**: a model emitted `Input["Amino-acid sequence"]` (mermaid-ish node labels) — INVALID Graphviz (viz-js: syntax error near ']'; the correct form is `Input [label="…"]`), verified against viz-js in Node. The entry now demonstrates `[label=…]` in its example and states the ban explicitly ("label nodes ONLY as ID [label=\"text\"]; bare ID[\"text\"] is INVALID Graphviz") — the hints philosophy's "better hint, not runtime detection"; no dot-side auto-repair (renderDot's error note + raw block stay the failure mode).
- History kept for context: ```nn shipped 2026-09-14 hand-rolled (NN-SVG's stack/fcnn visual languages, pure SVG) and drifted once (AGENT_RENDER_HINT missed it a day). `_rasterizeSvg` — extracted back then for nn/chem PNG exports — SURVIVES (smiles/pdb/reaction exports still use it); only the nn renderer/parser/CSS (`.nn-block`) and its test file are gone.

### Renderer-vendor audit (2026-09-29 — "业内通用优先" sweep, user-directed)

- **渲染器 vendor 审计（2026-09-29）** — 裁决：**行业通用优先**。审计后**保留**的每一项都有长期理由、无新事实勿重提：markmap（markdown→思维导图事实标准，用户复核过流行度后保留）、markstream 分页揭示器（无主流等价物）、`sanitizeMermaidSvg`（安全 + 退化渲染拒绝，DOMPurify 无等价能力）、`renderMathInPlainText` 的手写定界扫描（因公式走 KaTeX worker 批量，auto-render 无异步版本）。`renderUserContent` 由 2026-09-26 决定锁定（刻意为非全量 Markdown）。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **Rendering-Pipeline-zh「渲染器 vendor 审计结论」**

### Universal-implementation audit round 2 (2026-09-29, whole-repo — SSE/protocol layer)

Second sweep under the same principle, extending past renderers into protocol/utility code. **Fixed: the four protocol event parsers + both Hermes hooks each extracted the SSE `data:` payload independently (first-line or per-line JSON.parse), which silently DROPS continuation lines when one JSON is split across several `data:` lines — legal per the SSE spec (values join with `\n`), unused by today's LLM providers but a real trap. One helper, `sseDataPayload(eventText)` (llm-client.js), now owns the extraction for all five sites; multi-data-line regression tests in llm-client-chat-reasoning.test.mjs.** A transcription typo introduced during that refactor (`d.reason` for `d.reasoning`) was caught by the existing alias test within one run — the lockstep suite doing exactly its job. Audited and KEPT: **eventsource-parser** (the Vercel-AI-SDK-standard SSE lib) not adopted — it adds BOM/retry/id-field handling no LLM provider emits, while our frame layer (CRLF normalize, abort plumbing, budget renegotiation, thinking state machine) is the most battle-tested code in the product; the spec gap that mattered is now closed inline. **FNV-1a hash** in attach-change-tracker stays (sync, no crypto.subtle async/permission surface; change detection only needs "did it change"). Date bucketing stays (already `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat`/`toLocale*` based). `escM` stays (markdown-specific escaping, not a generic HTML-escape wheel). IDs already use `crypto.randomUUID`/platform `Math.random`. No other hand-rolled utility with a mainstream replacement surfaced (no custom debounce/deep-clone/escapeHtml).

**Dependency-popularity pass (same day, extends the audit to "niche lib where a mainstream one exists")**: every npm dep + vendor bundle checked against its category's mainstream alternatives — verdict: zero swaps. Notables with real numbers (monthly npm downloads, 2026-09): marked 280M / DOMPurify 236M / hljs 131M / KaTeX 94M / turndown 34M / turndown-plugin-gfm 5.2M — all category top-tier; **stream-markdown-parser is 265K/month (NOT the niche package the round-1 note implied)**; markmap ~350K (ratified); markstream-core 76K; @firecrawl/pdf-inspector-wasm 37K. The one genuinely small package is **docling.rs-wasm (6.3K/month)** — kept deliberately: its category (browser-side office→Markdown with table/heading fidelity) has NO mainstream equivalent; the named alternatives each cover a slice (mammoth: docx-only, tables degrade; SheetJS: spreadsheets only) while docling.rs-wasm is the wasm build of IBM's docling, the category's reference implementation. Category "two mainstreams" picks (marked-vs-markdown-it, hljs-vs-prism, KaTeX-vs-MathJax, echarts-vs-chart.js) all landed on a top-2 option — no niche preference anywhere.

### Full-scan hardening pass (2026-09-30, 批A–E — four-agent audit, user-directed 「都做了」)

A four-way audit sweep (perf/memory · bugs/correctness · refactor/dead-code · UI-UX/i18n) produced ~40 verified NEW findings; all landed in one pass. The invariants future edits must keep:

**批A (correctness):**
- **Interrupted-turn salvage accounting (P0, was reachable data loss)**: chat-handler's AbortError catch persists the partial reply as an `interrupted:true` entry (the `interrupted` field itself remains write-only — the entry renders as a normal assistant turn). Its visible twin MUST be stamped: user-initiated cancel stamps the cancelled bubble in `cancelStream` (only when `salvage && !detach && state.acc` non-empty — mirroring the background's `partial` gate); a background-initiated abort (idle timeout / network drop) reaches the still-connected port as `ERROR {code:'ABORTED', salvaged:true}` and wireChatStreamPort stamps there. An unstamped salvage makes storage LONGER than the hidx mirror (negative drift) and every later delete/edit-resend hits the WRONG entry. `planHistoryReconcile` returns `{action:'reset'}` for negative drift as a backstop (a uniform shift is wrong — bubbles stamped before the invisible append are correct); reset-only stops the compounding, data.ok-guarded deletes degrade the rest to no-ops. `activeController` now carries `{tabId, state}` on BOTH construction sites for this.
- **Stream tabId ownership**: STREAM_ABORT / REASSIGN_STREAM_SESSION address the stream's OWN tab (`streamTabIdOf()` = `activeController.tabId ?? currentTabId`), never bare `currentTabId` — the panel document survives tab switches, so Esc-on-tab-B used to silently no-op against tab A's still-burning server-side run. onSend pins ONE `turnTabId` (captured at port-open) through HELLO + CHAT + activeController.
- **PEEK→HELLO resume race**: `pushChunk` tombstones every foreground terminal event (`lib/state.js` `terminalTombstones` — 30s TTL, read-once via `takeTerminalTombstone`, cleared by `initStreamState` so a new turn can never inherit the old turn's DONE). A STREAM_HELLO carrying `resume:true` (set ONLY by resumeInFlightStream) whose streamState is already gone replays the tombstone — the REAL DONE/ERROR with its full payload — onto the new port; no tombstone (unreachable in practice) falls back to synthetic `{type:'DONE', full:null, synthetic:true}` and the panel finalizes with its PEEK-seeded acc (the turn is persisted by then, so DONE's hidxAssign is correct).
