History — figure pixels included — rides to the model verbatim on every turn; later turns keep seeing figure pixels (the old "labels only" tradeoff is REVERSED), and provider prefix caches stay hit across turns. `compactEntryImageParts`/`parseFigureLabels` survive solely as `boundUnseenImageBytes`'s undecodable-payload fallback (storage-side display). ADR-0005 unchanged.
- **Agent providers need zero change**: `buildAgentTurn` only collects the trailing page-context run (never answered → never flagged), so its image forwarding is naturally identical to the old behavior.
- **Storage side (panel-open hygiene)**: `boundUnseenImageBytes` caps parked pixels at `UNSEEN_IMAGE_BYTES_BUDGET` (8MB) by **downscaling** the oldest image-bearing entries' parts to display thumbnails (`downscaleDataUrl` — service-worker `createImageBitmap` + `OffscreenCanvas`, `THUMB_MAX_EDGE` 480px, opaque-white JPEG) — never to labels: an entry that loses its pixels loses its bubble picture. The newest image-bearing entry is always spared (the attach the user is about to ask about keeps full pixels for the model's first look). Only an undecodable payload falls back to label placeholders. The whole pass is ONE locked async read-modify-write (mutateHistory awaits the mutator); the earlier two-phase "plan victims by URL, rewrite by URL" design was tried and REJECTED — entries sharing one data URL over-matched and non-victims got rewritten (victim choice + rewrite must be atomic against concurrent appends/deletes).
- **Testing**: `test/history-compactor.test.mjs` covers the pure policy (`parseFigureLabels` / `compactEntryImageParts` / `prepareHistoryForModel`, incl. the identity-passthrough + no-flag-leak pins), `markImagesSeenInHistory` (stamp once, pixels untouched, idempotent), `boundUnseenImageBytes` (thumbnail semantics via an injected `downscale`, label fallback on undecodable payloads, newest spared, idempotent), and builder integration (`buildMessages` / `buildAnthropicMessages` / `buildRunsConversationHistory` — seen→labels, unseen→pixels, flag never on the wire). Related display fix in the same batch: `renderHistory` now joins ALL text parts of a multimodal user entry (it read only the first, silently dropping everything after an image).

### Background streams across session switches (2026-09-24)

Switching conversations mid-turn **no longer cancels the reply** (user report: "AI 还在 thinking，一切换就全没了" — a minutes-long reasoning run evaporated on a stray click). The turn keeps running in the service worker and its output lands in the session it STARTED in.

- **Routing rule (the whole design in one line)**: a turn entry is written to the LIVE history only while the panel is watching its stream (`streamState.bg === false`); once the stream is backgrounded it writes to the ORIGIN session's snapshot (`storage.appendToSessionHistory`). `chat-handler.persistTurnEntry(tabId, entry)` is the single writer — `bg` is set/cleared only by explicit messages (REASSIGN after a switch-away auto-save / STREAM_PEEK attach on a switch back), which kills the loadSession-vs-DONE race window (a mis-route can only go to the safe snapshot side).
- **Plumbing**: `initStreamState(tabId, { originSessionId })` stamps the turn's owning session (the sidepanel puts `sessionId` on the CHAT message); `REASSIGN_STREAM_SESSION` backgrounds the stream and re-stamps the origin to the switch-away auto-save's session id (SAVE_SESSION returns the session); `STREAM_PEEK` with `sessionId` re-attaches only when the session IS the origin (else the reply of ANOTHER conversation must never render here — `peek.bg` gates `resumeInFlightStream`).
- **Stop-watching ≠ cancel**: `cancelStream({ detach: true })` (exported as `stopWatchingStream`) does the local teardown (port detach, bubble finalize) WITHOUT `STREAM_ABORT` — the server-side turn continues. Plain `cancelStream()` (Esc / ■) still aborts, and `clearChatHistory` aborts with `salvage: false` (explicit destruction). `pushChunk` drops CHUNK/DONE/ERROR for bg streams (the panel is showing another conversation) but still pushes APPROVAL/CLARIFY — a blocked tool call must not hang the turn.
- **收尸 (interrupted-turn salvage, folded in here)**: an aborted turn with streamed text is persisted as `{ interrupted: true }` into the origin (user Esc / idle timeout / network drop — thinking must not evaporate); `abort('drop')` skips it. This replaced the old "abort = never write history" contract; the AbortError-throw contract on the CLIENTS is unchanged (a resolved-with-partial would still look like a completed turn).
- **UI**: the sessions drawer marks in-flight turns with a `.session-active-dot` (GET_SESSIONS gains `active` from live streams' origins); switching away toasts `streamMovedToBackground`; switching back auto-reattaches rendering. Known limit: `markImagesSeenInHistory` still walks only the live history, so a backgrounded turn's images get flagged one turn late (sends pixels once more — cost only, safe).
- **Tests**: `test/lib-persist-turn-routing.test.mjs` (live vs snapshot vs missing-session fallback, pushChunk bg gating, interrupted shape — `appendToSessionHistory` exercised via the bg route), `test/lib-sessions-ui.test.mjs` (loadSession sends REASSIGN + stop-watching and NEVER STREAM_ABORT).

### Message fold (collapse long replies, sidepanel.css + sidepanel.js)

Every `.done` assistant bubble gets a `.msg-actions` bar (absolutely positioned top-right, hover-revealed) whose fold button toggles `.collapsed` — purely visual and ephemeral (never persisted; `renderHistory` rebuilds fresh). The collapsed state is `max-height: 150px + overflow-y: hidden` with a bottom fade gradient (`.collapsed::after`). **The expand affordance lives INSIDE the window**: the action bar is pinned at `top: 4px`, so the fold button stays within the visible 150px — never move the bar to the bubble bottom, or a collapsed bubble loses its only expand control. **And it must be VISIBLE when collapsed, not hover-gated** (2026-09-23 user report): the bar's `.msg:hover` reveal turns a folded bubble into a dead end whenever something below it (an inline 追问 card) jumps up under the pointer on collapse — `:hover` stops matching the bubble and the icon "never comes out". `.msg.assistant.collapsed .msg-actions .fold-btn` is therefore exempted from the hover gate (persistently visible); the rest of the bar keeps the hover-only rule (unread-text occlusion).

**Flexbox squash incident (2026-09-14, user report: collapsed messages became ~4px slivers, impossible to re-open)**: `.messages` is a fixed-height flex column (`overflow-y: auto`), and flex items default to `flex-shrink: 1`. Ordinary bubbles are protected by the `min-height: auto` content floor — but a flex item whose `overflow` is NOT visible **loses that floor**, so the collapsed bubble (overflow-y:hidden) became the only shrinkable item and absorbed the container's ENTIRE shrink deficit once the history exceeded the viewport: rendered as its 8px padding, invisible, and its hover-only action bar clipped away. Fix: `flex-shrink: 0` on the base `.msg` rule (items in a scrollable list are content-sized, always). Lesson: in a fixed-height flex column, ANY item that ever gets non-visible overflow needs an explicit `flex-shrink: 0`. jsdom has no flex layout, so the contract is pinned structurally in `test/lib-message-fold-css.test.mjs` (base rule carries flex-shrink:0; collapsed = max-height + overflow-Y only; action bar top-pinned).

### Page inline images (reader/auto/jina 配图 [图N] 内联, `lib/page-images.js`, 2026-08-29)

Reader-mode Markdown already carries article images **in position** — Turndown emits `![alt](url)` (with page-extractor's srcset→best-src upgrade), and even Jina output does. `inlinePageImages` (called in `ATTACH_PAGE` for `['reader','auto','jina']`, after `withSiteInstructions`) downloads those images in the service worker, compresses each to a ≤768px JPEG dataURL (`createImageBitmap` + `OffscreenCanvas` — both available in MV3 SW; white-fill first or transparent PNGs turn black under JPEG), and rewrites each kept image **in place** to a `[图N] alt` anchor line. The entry then stores `interleaveImageParts(contextText, figures)` — the exact same [图N] protocol as video keyframes (`ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM`), so output-side `decorateFigureRefs` renders model citations of page figures with zero extra wiring.

- **Cap/filter**: max 8 figures (resend-every-turn token ceiling), 16 candidates scanned; per-image ≥200×120 (icons/avatars/dividers drop by decoded size), 8s fetch timeout, 8MB download cap, non-`image/*` content types rejected. Same URL anchors only its first occurrence; numbering follows document order with no gaps — `figures[N-1]` must correspond to `[图N]`.
- **Fail-open everywhere**: any download/decode failure keeps that image's `![alt](url)` verbatim (the model still sees the URL); no decode environment (non-SW) or zero successes returns the text unchanged and the entry stays a plain-string content — the pre-existing shape. SVG fails decode in Chrome and self-filters.
- **Scope**: `dom`/`full` modes are tree-format text (no Markdown image syntax) and `selected` is a partial excerpt — all excluded. XHS keeps its own `imageBase64List` vision path. Lazy-loaded below-fold images whose `src` is a tiny placeholder drop via the size filter (acceptable V1 loss).
- **Interplay**: history image compaction labels these `[image N]` (same numbering as the [图N] anchors, non-PDF branch); auto-summarize preserves the `image_url` blocks like every other multimodal entry. Tests: `test/lib-page-images.test.mjs` (pure, stubbed decoder) + `test/attach-page-images.test.mjs` (wiring via the jina branch — no DOM mocking needed).

### X/Twitter syndication fallback (推文 URL 免登录兜底, 2026-10-16 用户报告)

**裁决：推文 URL（`x.com|twitter.com/…/status/<id>`）的提取不得落到 r.jina.ai**——jina 是匿名服务端抓取，X 的推文页只给登录墙（Log in/Sign up 噪声 + 全部图片丢失）。取而代之的两级权威数据：① 页内 `activeXFetch`（登录页的 `__INITIAL_STATE__`/DOM，含可见回复与图片字节）；② **`cdn.syndication.twimg.com/tweet-result` 兜底**（`fetchTweetViaSyndication`，X 自家 embed 数据源：免登录免 Cookie 免 DOM，全文/作者/计数/mediaDetails 原图 URL；token 是 react-tweet 同款公开公式 `(id/1e15)*π` 六进制去零，非鉴权；`display_text_range` 截掉 text 尾部模型打不开的 t.co 短链；照片 SW 侧下载压缩随 `imageBase64List` 入库，端点不含可见回复——回复以页内路径优先）。接线两处：`tryXActiveFallback` 的 null 兜底（auto/reader/dom/full 级联由此受益）+ background 显式 jina 模式的推文 URL 抢占（拿不到才退回 jina，不困死用户）。**auto 的 jina 回退判据已正解化为 `autoJinaFallbackWarranted`（page-extractor 纯函数，2026-10-16 用户问「怎样算提取失败」）**：绝对下限（<200 字符，原有救援全保留）+ 相对亏空（`body.innerText` 量 >2000 且提取量不足两成 → 失败——付费墙/重 JS 页的救援反而变多，旧判据下 400 字符的半失败不触发；innerText 而非 textContent——后者把脚本字面量算进去，任何提取都像亏空）；`ctx.siteSynthesized`（makeSynthResult 单点标记）永不回退——短推文/短帖天然短，长度不是提取失败的证据（同日用户抓到的残余 bug：级联测试用的 dom 模式恰好没打中 auto 这条门）。探针不可用（tab 关/注入抛）→ 相对层失效、绝对层照旧。行为地板（变异验证×5：删兜底/删抢占/改坏 token 公式/拆 siteSynthesized 门/拆相对亏空层各红其位）：`attach-site-images.test.mjs` 的「X syndication 兜底」节（token 字面量钉死 + 短推文 auto 不回退）+ `site-synthesizers.test.mjs` 的 `twitterFromSyndication` 映射用例（display_text_range 截链、photos `?name=large`、video-only 提示行、垃圾载荷 null）。

### Paper reading pipeline (page markers, outline, arXiv metadata, reading-aid card, 2026-08-31)

Research-paper PDFs get a dedicated quality pass across four layers, all best-effort/fail-open — a miss at any layer leaves the pre-existing attach behavior untouched.

- **`[Page N]` markers in both extraction paths** (`lib/sidepanel/pdf-extractor.js`): the wasm engine is called with `includePageMarkers: true` and its `<!-- Page N -->` comments are normalized to visible `[Page N]` lines (`normalizePageMarkers`, pure + tested — dedupes a page boundary the engine emits twice); the pdf.js fallback stamps the same marker per page. Page-boundary-less text was the root cause of two model-facing gaps: no page-precise citations and only weak "Figure on page N" figure anchoring. The markers also give the auto-summarizer something to preserve (see paper-mode prompts below).
- **Layout-aware fallback text** (`reconstructPageText`, pure + tested): the old `items.map(str).join(' ')` emitted content-stream order, which scrambles every two-column paper into interleaved column fragments. It now clusters runs into lines by baseline y (tolerance 0.3× font size), splits a line into column bands on gaps > 12% of page width, and emits each band top-to-bottom (left band first). Single-column pages are unchanged in spirit; whitespace-only runs drop.
- **Bookmarks → `## Contents`** (`flattenOutline`/`formatOutlineMarkdown` + `extractPdfOutline`, tested): `doc.getOutline()` was never read. Outline items' `dest` (array `[pageRef, /XYZ,…]` or named string) resolves to 1-indexed pages matching the `[Page N]` markers; unresolvable dests keep the title with `page: null`. Prepended to the body only when ≥ 3 entries exist (a lone "Cover" node is not a TOC). Concurrency: wasm text, outline, and figures are three INDEPENDENT passes (each opens its own pdf.js doc / worker call from a fresh byte copy) and run CONCURRENTLY via `raceWithTimeout` (outline 15s, figures 90s, timer always cleared — a lingering setTimeout stalls Node's test runner); wall-clock = slowest single pass, not the sum (an earlier version serialized outline after wasm and figures after that, visibly slowing every attach — timings logged as `browsa[pdf]: extract done in Nms` + `attach pipeline (extract+arxiv) took Nms`).
- **arXiv metadata header** (`lib/arxiv.js`, tested): `arxivIdFromUrl` handles `/abs/`, `/pdf/` (± `.pdf`, ± `vN`), `/html/`, old-style `cs/0301012` IDs across `arxiv.org`/`export.arxiv.org`. `fetchArxivMeta` calls the Atom API (`export.arxiv.org/api/query?id_list=…`, 6s timeout) and parses via DOMParser with a regex fallback (DOMParser is absent in Node tests and the regex path must match the DOM result). `formatArxivMeta` renders the header block; the sidepanel fires it **in parallel with extraction**, and `ATTACH_PDF_CONFIRM` prepends it to the context header (never into the body text). Non-arXiv/network failure → `null` → no block. The attach system message shows `，arXiv:<id>` as visible confirmation (the 检查 dialog displays the FULL `contextText` returned by `ATTACH_PDF_CONFIRM` — header + body + Figures — not the pre-header pdfText the sidepanel holds).
- **Paper reading-aid card — REMOVED (2026-08-31, user decision)**: an earlier iteration ran a one-shot structured analysis (concept chips / TLDR / digest) via `chatStream` against the active provider after paper-shaped attaches. Dropped for two compounding reasons: the user's active provider is Hermes, whose `runsApiStream` wire protocol the card did not speak (it silently skipped on non-chat providers — invisible failure), and the value add over the already-attached full text was marginal against the protocol-branching cost. Lesson recorded: **any sidepanel feature that makes its own LLM call must either support all four API styles (chat / runs / responses / anthropic) or fail VISIBLY, not silently.** The protocol-agnostic layers above (markers, outline, arXiv header, paper-mode summarizer prompts) remain.
- **Paper-mode auto-summarize prompts** (`attach-summarizer.js`): `summarizeLongText` now accepts the full `ctx` (bare mode strings still work — video behavior byte-identical); when `ctx.meta.paper`, the chunk prompt preserves section headings + `[Page N]` markers + concrete numbers and drops citation lists, and the merge prompt organizes by the paper's own headings. The stored `pdfCtx.meta.paper` flag rides the same `maybeSummarizeAttachment` path as before.
- **Testing**: pure functions live in `lib/arxiv.js` and pdf-extractor's exported helpers — covered by `test/lib-arxiv.test.mjs` (10 cases) and `test/lib-pdf-structure.test.mjs` (7 cases). The DOM/streaming paths follow the established "manual browser sanity check" convention.

### Content scripts

Site-specific interceptors (`lib/content-scripts/*-content-script.js`) run in **MAIN world** at `document_start` and wrap `window.fetch` / `XMLHttpRequest` to capture SPA API responses. They send messages to the background, which stores results in `SITE_CACHES` (a registry object in `lib/handlers/site-cache-store.js`（2026-09-23 C7 自 background.js 外迁） — one `Map` per site, keyed by `tabId`). Adding a new site only requires a single entry in `SITE_CACHES`; restore, lookup, and cleanup iterate it automatically. XHS is the one exception — it uses a separate `xhsXhrCache` Map with its own push logic instead of a `SITE_CACHES` entry. `lib/content-scripts/selection-toolbar.js` runs in ISOLATED world at `document_idle` on all `https://` pages.

MAIN-world content scripts can't use ES module imports, so URL matchers there must resolve relative paths against the page: use `new URL(url, location.origin)`, not `new URL(url)` — a bare relative fetch/XHR path (e.g. `fetch('/api/...')`) throws and gets silently swallowed otherwise.

Extension resources used inside Shadow DOM or injected pages (e.g. `icons/icon16.png`) must be declared in `web_accessible_resources`. `ShadowRoot` supports both `querySelector('#id')` and `getElementById('id')` in current Chrome — prefer `querySelector` for consistency with the rest of the codebase.

### Selection toolbar inline explain/translate (划词内联解释/翻译, 2026-09-06)

The float bar's 「解释」 and 「翻译」 no longer route through `SELECTION_ACTION` into the side panel — both stream their answer into an **inline popover anchored next to the selection** (product form validated against 沉浸式翻译's on-demand selection/hover translation, PickZ, and Saladict: the answer appears in place, reading flow unbroken; the side panel stays reserved for conversational actions — Ask/Summarize keep the old relay, per the inline-vs-panel rule: 看完就走 → 浮条；要说话/要留存/要工具 → 侧栏). Design constraints baked into the implementation:

- **Streaming leg is a one-shot port** (`browsa-explain`), not `sendMessage`: the content script connects, sends `{type:'EXPLAIN_REQUEST', text, lang}` as the first message, gets `EXPLAIN_CHUNK`/`EXPLAIN_DONE`/`EXPLAIN_ERROR` back. No HELLO handshake needed (unlike browsa-chat/subchat) — `connect()` itself wakes the SW and `onConnect` necessarily registers listeners before any port message arrives, so the reconnect race those handshakes guard against can't exist here. Closing the popover = `port.disconnect()` → background's `AbortController.abort()` kills the upstream fetch (the ONLY dismissal path that cancels the request; scroll/resize merely re-anchor).
- **`lib/handlers/selection-explain.js`** owns the session: `buildExplainRequest(text, lang, mode)` (pure, tested) + `handleExplainPort(port, deps)` (deps-injectable `getAll`/`streams` for tests — no chrome mock needed). Fixed `EXPLAIN_MAX_TOKENS = 700` — deliberately NOT `provider.maxTokens` (that's the chat-output budget, irrelevant to a 150-char dictionary entry) and NOT `DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS`; anthropicStream needs a mandatory value, this always provides one. apiStyle dispatch mirrors `mermaid-repair.js` (chat/responses/anthropic — Hermes providers also work since the server natively serves `/v1/chat/completions`). Answer language follows the settings `uiLang` override, else browser language, threaded from the content script as `lang`; in `mode: 'translate'` the TARGET language flips zh↔en when the selection is already (mostly) in the UI language (`isMostlyCjk`, CJK-vs-latin counting) — a zh user selecting Chinese gets English and vice versa. Port protocol keeps the `EXPLAIN_*` message names for both modes (renaming to `INLINE_*` is pure churn); the request carries `mode`.
- **Two closed shadow hosts** in selection-toolbar.js (`#browsa-sel-host` for the bar + `#browsa-sel-pop-host` for the popover): independent fixed positioning, same palette incl. the `prefers-color-scheme: light` variant. Popover anchors to the SELECTION (opposite side from the bar, so the highlighted text stays visible; the bar's `.below` flip flips the popover too), re-anchors on scroll/resize via the stored `Range.getBoundingClientRect()` — a long explanation must survive scrolling, unlike the bar which hides.
- **Model output never touches `innerHTML`**: `renderExplainBody` is a deliberately tiny renderer (bold/code inline tokens, `-`/`•`/`1.` bullets, line breaks) that builds nodes with `createTextNode`/`textContent` only — XSS-safe by construction; unparseable markup degrades to literal text. The system prompt simultaneously forbids tables/headings/images so the renderer's poverty isn't visible.
- The bar's document-level guards (`mouseup`/`mousedown` composedPath checks, right-click collapse, Esc, scroll, ✕-suppress) all learned `popHost` — selecting text INSIDE the popover must not reposition the bar, and right-click inside it must not kill the explanation mid-copy. `GET_PENDING_ACTION`/context-menu flows are untouched; the popover's 「在侧栏细聊」 deliberately sends the OLD `SELECTION_ACTION {action:'chat'}` (attach + focus composer, no auto-send).
- Tests: `test/selection-explain.test.mjs` (prompt building, apiStyle dispatch, error envelopes, single-session guard, disconnect→abort).

### YouTube content script critical lessons (confirmed 2026-07-25)

- **`chrome.runtime` is undefined in MAIN world** — `installYouTubeInterceptor()` must NOT guard on `chrome.runtime`; use only `typeof window !== 'undefined'`.
- **YouTube loads the current video's captions via XHR, not fetch** — `XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send` must be wrapped to intercept `/api/timedtext`. The fetch wrapper only catches recommended/sidebar video pre-fetches (whose responses are often HTML error pages or empty).
- **timedtext URLs require a PO token (`exp=xpe`) — but only the WEB-client ones** — re-fetching the watch page's own caption URL (`ytInitialPlayerResponse`, and therefore the `performance` resource-buffer entries) returns 200 + empty body. Verified 2026-09-10 that this is client-specific, not universal: the caption `baseUrl` returned by the **ANDROID** `/youtubei/v1/player` POST carries no `exp=xpe` (it does carry `fmt=srv3`) and returns the full body to a bare `fetch` with no UA, no cookies and no token (11,536 bytes of srv3). That is the URL `activeYouTubeFetch` Stage 3 already uses, so the transcript path does not actually depend on the XHR interception's PO token — the interception is a page-load-time head start, not the only route. (The interception is still what feeds `__browsaTranscriptCache` passively, and remains worth keeping.)
- **Do not modify `fmt=` in the baseUrl** — even though `fmt` is not in `sparams`, re-fetching with a modified URL still fails. Parse the original response body directly (supports both json3 and srv3/ttml XML).
- **字幕文本带标记，必须在离开解析器前归一化**（2026-09-10 用户报告「原生视频字幕带 `<b>`」）— 裁决：timedtext 的文本**逐字带标记**，解析后统一剥标签 + 实体解码；越界或孤立代理项的数字实体**丢弃而不是抛错**（一个坏实体不得让整条字幕变 null）。重写是旧时间戳处理的超集（`<p>` 与 `<text>` 都经同一个 `parseSubtitleStart` 接受 `t`/`tStartMs`/`start`/`begin`），**绝不比之前少提取一行**。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **ASR-and-Video-Analysis-zh「字幕文本的标记归一化」**
- **`/player` uses ANDROID client, `/next` uses WEB client** — ANDROID `/next` returns `singleColumnWatchNextResults` with `elementRenderer` blobs (unparseable). Fire both in parallel via `Promise.allSettled`.
- **YouTube SITE_CACHES is always skipped** — the passive interceptor runs in MAIN world without `chrome.runtime`, so `SITE_CACHES` never gets YouTube data. `page-extractor.js` always falls through to `activeYouTubeFetch`.

### X/Twitter tweet extraction — active fallback (`tryXActiveFallback`)

X/Twitter content scripts had the **same passive-interceptor failure as YouTube**: `twitter-content-script.js` is declared in the manifest with `world: MAIN`, and its `installTwitterInterceptor()` guards on `chrome.runtime` — which is **undefined in MAIN world** — so it never installs and `SITE_CACHES.twitter` is never populated. A tweet page therefore fell through to the generic DOM-tree dump (`extractDomTreeInPageWorld`), which for X's div-based layout includes the whole nav/trending/sidebar chrome (Grok/Chat buttons, "Skip to home timeline", "What's happening") — the user-facing bug this fixes.

- **Fix**: `tryXActiveFallback(tab, meta)` in `lib/page-extractor.js` (wired into `extractActiveTab` right after the bilibili fallback, matching the `tryYoutubeActiveFallback`/`tryBilibiliActiveFallback` pattern) injects `twitter-content-script.js` on-demand via `chrome.scripting.executeScript({ world: 'MAIN', files: [...] })`, then calls the new MAIN-world `activeXFetch()`. It reads the tweet + visible replies out of `window.__INITIAL_STATE__.entities.tweets/users` (X embeds the conversation in SSR state, same idea as Bilibili/XHS), falling back to the rendered DOM's stable `[data-testid="tweet"]` / `[data-testid="tweetText"]` / `[data-testid="User-Name"]` attributes when the state object is absent/stale. `synthesizeTwitterResult` now also emits a `## 回复` numbered conversation from the `replies` array (backwards-compatible: old XHR shape with only `data.replies` count still works).
- **llms.txt side-fix** (`fetchLlmsTxt` in `lib/handlers/chat-handler.js`): x.com serves its SPA index.html with HTTP 200 + `Content-Type: text/html` for `/llms.txt`, which used to be treated as "site instructions" and injected hundreds of KB of raw HTML into the page context. The fetch now rejects any `text/html` Content-Type, and (when no Content-Type header is present) rejects a body that starts with `<`.
- **Gotcha**: in `auto` mode, the silent Jina fallback (< 200 chars) can override a *short* tweet's active-fallback result — acceptable: Jina's fetch of a tweet URL returns reasonable tweet markdown, and long tweets keep the structured X path. The `tryXActiveFallback` URL gate matches `twitter.com`/`x.com` `/status/` paths only (not home/search).

### Reddit post+comments extraction — active fallback (`tryRedditActiveFallback`)

Reddit has **no passive content-script interceptor** — it's a componentized SPA with no simple XHR endpoint to hook (unlike Twitter's GraphQL call), so pages used to fall through to the generic DOM-tree dump, which for Reddit's div/web-component layout pulled in the whole chrome: skip links (跳到主要内容/跳至导航/跳至右侧边栏), vote/share/reward buttons, ads (`alb.reddit.com` links), footer links, stray `— Item N —` repeated-group markers, and the raw `SML.load([...])` module-loader strings Reddit's comments leak into their `<summary>` text.

- **Fix**: `tryRedditActiveFallback(tab, meta)` in `lib/page-extractor.js` (wired into `extractActiveTab` right after the X/Twitter fallback) injects `lib/content-scripts/reddit-content-script.js` on-demand via `chrome.scripting.executeScript({ world: 'MAIN', files: [...] })`, then calls the new MAIN-world `activeRedditFetch()`. It reads the post + comments out of `window.__INITIAL_STATE__` (Reddit embeds SSR state — probes `posts.posts`, `profiles.profiles`, `users.users` paths defensively since the shape is route-dependent and has changed over time), falling back to the DOM: `shreddit-post`/`shreddit-comment` web components (author/score/depth live in attributes), then a generic `<h1>`-based post + `<details>/<summary>` comment structure (the exact shape the old DOM dump showed). `stripSmlNoise` removes the `SML.load([...])` leak before synthesis. `synthesizeRedditResult` emits `# title`, a `r/sub · u/author · score · comments` meta line, the body, and a depth-indented `## 评论` tree.
- **No manifest entry needed**: the script is purely an active-fallback library (no auto-install at document_start), and `chrome.scripting.executeScript({ files: [...] })` can inject any packaged path. `.json` API routes are NOT used — Reddit blocks bare server-side fetches (403) and a SW fetch wouldn't carry the user's login cookies anyway; the in-page DOM/SSR path does.
- **Fail-open**: if neither INITIAL_STATE nor the DOM yields a recognizable post, it returns null and falls through to the generic cascade — never a regression from current behavior.

### Video ASR subtitles (multi-provider: 阿里云百炼 qwen + 火山方舟 Ark) — module map

**Module layout (2026-10-07 tidy, user-directed):** the ASR domain is three files behind ONE facade — `attach-asr.js` (facade: registry, asrAdapterFor, upload-file cache, transcode, transcript post-processing, the ARK adapter, and re-exports of every other provider's symbols so callers/tests keep importing from `attach-asr.js`), `attach-asr-qwen.js` (the whole Bailian adapter: upload/omni-chat/filetrans/ping + `QWEN_DEFAULT_BASE_URL`), and `asr-shared.js` (provider-neutral contract layer: `streamResponsesText` + SSE extractors, the transcribe/video prompt builders, coverage hints, `transcriptEndSec`/`largestTranscriptGapSec`/`parseTranscriptTimeline`/`formatStampSec`). Dependency direction is one-way: qwen → shared, ark/facade → shared + qwen. When editing a prompt builder or the streaming parser you are editing the SHARED contract — rerun lib-attach-asr + lib-asr-qwen (the ASI-truncation lesson below was caught exactly there).

Attach a subtitle-less **Bilibili or YouTube** video and, when 多模态视频理解 is enabled in options, browsa downloads the audio in-page and runs the selected provider's ASR to produce a `[mm:ss] text` transcript. **Why download→upload-file, not URL direct-pass**: the openspeech `auc/bigmodel` endpoint (`openspeech.bytedance.com/api/v3/auc/bigmodel/submit`) only accepts a public `audio.url` (URL direct-pass) — but Bilibili signature URLs bind to the user's session IP, so the Volcengine server can't fetch them (the original 08-06/07 failure). Ark's Files API (`POST {baseUrl}/files`, multipart, ≤512MB) accepts uploaded bytes instead, so browsa fetches the audio **in-page** (user IP matches the signature / browser session) and uploads the bytes — bypassing the binding at the root. **URL direct-pass is dead for BOTH platforms**: Bilibili = IP binding, YouTube = PO token + session + cookie binding (server-side fetch would 403); the download→transcode→upload-file path is the only viable route, not an alternative to it. Bailian's filetrans RESTful is the one documented exception that accepts `oss://` temp URLs (still fed from our own upload).

Attach a subtitle-less **Bilibili or YouTube** video and, when ASR is enabled in options, browsa downloads the audio in-page and runs 火山方舟 (Volcengine Ark) 录音文件识别 to produce a `[mm:ss] text` transcript. **Why Ark, not openspeech**: the openspeech `auc/bigmodel` endpoint (`openspeech.bytedance.com/api/v3/auc/bigmodel/submit`) only accepts a public `audio.url` (URL direct-pass) — but Bilibili signature URLs bind to the user's session IP, so the Volcengine server can't fetch them (the original 08-06/07 failure). Ark's Files API (`POST {baseUrl}/files`, multipart, ≤512MB) accepts uploaded bytes instead, so browsa fetches the audio **in-page** (user IP matches the signature / browser session) and uploads the bytes — bypassing the binding at the root. **URL direct-pass is dead for BOTH platforms**: Bilibili = IP binding, YouTube = PO token + session + cookie binding (server-side fetch would 403); the download→transcode→upload-file path is the only viable route, not an alternative to it.

- **Pipeline** (mirrors the `pdf-pending` handoff): `ATTACH_PAGE` returns `{mode:'asr-pending'}` (deferred storage) → sidepanel runs download→transcode→upload, then polls + transcribes (sidepanel has a window, unlike the SW, and `host_permissions` let the extension fetch Ark with no CORS) → `ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM` stores the transcript with `videoSrc` stamped so `[mm:ss]` renders as clickable seek links.
- **`buildAsrPendingCtx`** (background.js): **platform-dispatch on `ctx.mode`** (bilibili vs youtube). **Bilibili**: re-injects `bilibili-content-script.js`, reads `window.__browsaGetBilibiliStreams()` (prefers fresh playurl on expiry). **YouTube**: re-injects `youtube-content-script.js`, calls the new MAIN-world `window.__browsaFetchFreshYouTubeStreams(videoId)` — a fresh `/youtubei/v1/player` ANDROID POST (mirroring `activeYouTubeFetch` Stage 3) whose response's `streamingData` carries a PO token / signature valid at download time (unlike the stale `ytInitialPlayerResponse` SSR global). Both paths then feed the SAME shared selection logic: pick the LOWEST-bitrate `type:'audio'` stream (quality is irrelevant — it gets transcoded to 16kHz mono WAV anyway) but REJECT streams whose `duration` metadata is < 90% of the video's true length (a truncated/partial stream — real bug: a 100+ min video once produced only ~20 min of subtitles; 90% tolerance matches the sidepanel WAV guard below), prefers decodable AAC-LC (`mp4a.40.2`) over HE-AAC (`mp4a.40.5`) which `decodeAudioData` may reject, and attaches the ASR config (`apiKey/baseUrl/model/language/format/timeoutMs`) to the ctx. Returns `null` (falls through to the normal store path) when no audio stream is available. The returned `asr-pending` ctx carries `asrPlatform` (the ORIGINAL mode, preserved across the `mode:'asr-pending'` rewrite) so the sidepanel knows which platform's DNR/headers/self-heal to use.
- **Detection keys off the structured `noTranscript` flag** on the synthesized result (added in `synthesizeBilibiliResult` AND `synthesizeYouTubeResult` — YouTube gained it in the same pass so ASR works there too), **NOT the `## 字幕`/`*(No captions...)*` text marker** — a real bug found via tests: auto mode's silent Jina fallback replaces the short (<200-char) subtitle-less synthesis with a full DOM dump, silently dropping any text marker. `Object.assign` in the Jina branch preserves the flag across the rewrite. `noTranscript: !data.transcript` survives to the `asr-pending` ctx for the fail-open fallback text.
- **`downloadAudioBytes` / `transcodeAudioBlob` / `uploadBlobToArk`** run in the sidepanel (extension context), NOT page-world: the Ark upload is a cross-origin request page-world JS cannot make (Ark sends no CORS headers; only an extension context with `host_permissions` is exempt — the original MAIN-world-injected version returned "Failed to fetch" on the upload, fixed 2026-08-15). `downloadAudioBytes` fetches the audio with `Referer` + `Range: bytes=0-` (some CDN paths 403 a Range-less request; the Referer is injected by the session DNR rule, not the header). **Both platforms DNR-inject now** — the early assumption that YouTube needed no headers (pot-in-URL auth) was WRONG: real test 2026-08-25 showed googlevideo 403s a plain extension-context fetch (chrome-extension Origin rejected) on EVERY fresh stream, even after `ASR_FRESH_URLS` re-signed. The sidepanel's session DNR rule now injects `Referer: https://www.youtube.com` + `Origin: https://www.youtube.com` (rewriting the chrome-extension Origin) + the YouTube cookie set (`SID`/`VISITOR_INFO1_LIVE`/etc., read by `buildAsrPendingCtx` via `chrome.cookies.getAll` exactly like bilibili's SESSDATA) scoped to `urlFilter: 'googlevideo'` + `initiatorDomains:[chrome.runtime.id]` — the mirror of the bilibili `bilivideo` rule. The bytes (a ~44MB audio → ~59MB base64 would break the 64MB messaging limit) never cross extension messaging. The three are orchestrated in `sidepanel.js`'s ASR branch, which reads `ctx.asrPlatform` to decide DNR rule, download headers, self-heal path, and platform labels (B站 vs YouTube).
- **`transcodeAudioBlob` — the 08-16 root-cause fix for file status `failed`**: a Bilibili m4s is an **MP4 container (fMP4, `ftyp=iso5`, pure audio no video track)**. Ark's Files API classifies files **by content** as "video", runs the video-preprocessing path, and a video-less MP4 fails with `InvalidArgumentError("Invalid video_url.")` → file status `failed` → the later Responses API transcription is rejected. Proven empirically (2026-08-16): a 16kHz mono WAV uploads to status `active` and transcribes end-to-end; the same bytes renamed `.m4a`/`.mp4`/any MIME + `preprocess_configs[video][fps]` still `failed`. So the bytes are **transcoded to 16kHz mono 16-bit PCM WAV** (a format Ark supports and can't misclassify as video): decode with Web Audio API `decodeAudioData` (Chrome decodes fMP4/AAC natively — prefers real `AudioContext`, falls back to `OfflineAudioContext`), then manual **linear-interpolation resample + stereo downmix via `resampleToMono`** (pure, Node-testable) → `encodePcmToWav`. 16kHz mono is the ASR-standard rate and keeps a 47-min video to ~32MB. `encodePcmToWav` (pure) writes a RIFF/WAVE header + clamps samples to [-1,1]; the resample being pure is deliberate — the decode path itself can't run in Node, so the algorithm that matters most (48k→16k) has real test coverage instead of only shape tests. A `>200MB` raw input fails open early (decode would otherwise hold ~390MB float32 in memory).
- **`pollFileStatus` / `transcribeAudio`** run in the sidepanel (extension context): plain `fetch` to `{baseUrl}/files/{id}` and `{baseUrl}/responses` with `input_audio.file_id` (NOT `input_file` — that's PDF-only, "type is not pdf"). The transcribe prompt forces a `[mm:ss] text` per-line output (matches `linkifyTimestamps`'s regex) and `formatAsrTranscript` defensively strips meta lines (`以下是…`/`以上是…`/`开始`/`注:`), list prefixes (`-`/`>`), and keeps bare text lines as a fallback.
- **Truncation defenses — never silently store a partial transcript** (three layers; real bug: a 52:48 video's context stopped at 33 min): (1) `transcribeAudio` sets `max_output_tokens: 65536` in the `/responses` body — a verbatim long-audio transcript far exceeds the model's DEFAULT output cap, and without this the doubao model just STOPS mid-audio (the 33/52.8 ≈ 62.5% cut, ~16K-token default) with no error; (2) it also parses the SSE for a truncation signal (`response.completed` with `status:'incomplete'` / `incomplete_details.reason:'max_output_tokens'`, or chat-compat `choices[0].finish_reason:'length'`) and returns `truncated:true` (non-stream JSON fallback checks `status:'incomplete'` the same way); (3) `sidepanel.js`'s ASR branch, after `formatAsrTranscript`, runs a **completeness guard**: if `tr.truncated` OR the transcript's last timestamp covers < 90% of the video (`transcriptEndSec`, a pure helper parsing `[mm:ss]`/`[mm:ss.mmm-mm:ss.mmm]` ranges/`[h:mm:ss]`/comma ranges `[mm:ss, mm:ss]`), it throws → fail-open to plain text + toast — the cut-off transcript is NEVER stored. **(4) hole guard (2026-08-30)**: a real 81-min video produced a doc covering 0-16 min + 49:27 + the outro — last stamp passed the 90% check while ~1h was missing in the middle. `largestTranscriptGapSec` (interval-merged timeline via `parseTranscriptTimeline`; range spans are coverage, not gaps) rejects docs whose biggest adjacent gap exceeds `TRANSCRIPT_GAP_LIMIT_SEC` (300s), with the offending span in the error. Both pipelines also log `usage` (captured from `response.completed` / non-stream `usage` in `streamResponsesText`) — the input_tokens figure tells whether the model actually ingested the full audio/video, which separates 'model skipped content' from 'server truncated input' when diagnosing partial coverage. Same 90% threshold is used by the WAV-duration guard (`wavDur < wantDurSec * 0.9`, was 50% — which passed a 62.5%-truncated audio stream) that retries the next candidate stream / fails loudly when every candidate is truncated.
- **Config** lives in `storage.js` DEFAULTS `asr` block (synced with `ASR_DEFAULTS` in `attach-asr.js`): `enabled`, `apiKey`, `baseUrl` (default `https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3`), `model` (default `doubao-seed-2-1-lite-260915`), `language` (default `zh`), `format` (default `audio/x-m4a` — superseded by the WAV transcode since 08-16; kept for the `downloadAndUploadAudio` legacy path), `timeoutMs`. Editable in options.html's ASR card (`applyAsr`/`saveAsr` in options.js following the `applyLimits`/`saveLimits` pattern; `saveAsr` requires an API key when enabling).
- **Fail-open**: any ASR failure (download error, transcode reject, upload reject, poll timeout, empty transcript) falls back to `ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM` with the plain video text — never a stuck state, matching the pre-existing behavior. `downloadAndUploadAudio` (legacy, no transcode) is kept for old callers/tests.
- **Ark Files 复用缓存 + 视听精读提示词纪律（2026-08-29/30）**：上传带 `expire_at`=now+30d（方舟上限，默认 7d）与语义化文件名；成功 file id 存 `browsaArkFileCache`（键 `baseUrl|apiKey指纹|assetId`），30 天内同视频重附加跳过上传（`lookupCachedArkFiles` → 逐文件 alive 探针 → 404/时长不符/过期一律 fail-open 走全路径）；音频模式命中连下载+转码都跳，视频模式仍要下 blob（关键帧截图需要）。**缓存按文件合并写**（只存视频时保住音频字段原 `expireAt`——复用的 id 绝不刷新过期时间，文件死于上传+30d）。提示词三条首轮实测喂出来的规则：任一第二人声出现就标说话人（插片/旁白/换语都算）；可辨认身份的说话人首行尾注名（`……（特朗普）`）；广告口播压成一行 `（广告：品牌+卖点）`、内部禁关键帧标记。**关键帧=只截关键图**（2026-08-30 两轮用户校准：固定 6 张太机械 → 不设上限出 37 张含片头/表情包/致谢卡 → 提示词写「只截论证依赖的画面」+ 软密度提示（15-20min ≈ 6-10）+ 显式不截清单；唯一硬上限是客户端 `SAFETY_KEYFRAME_CAP=24`——病态输出保险丝，不是质量旋钮）。`parseKeyframeMarkers` 返回幸存者的原始 `line`，面板**只重编号幸存者**（全量重编号曾产出无图可对的 [图25]+ 锚）。`manifest.json` 因此加 `unlimitedStorage`（历史条目里的未封顶 keyframe/页面图 dataURL 曾可达默认 10MB 配额）。**2026-08-30 格式回归**（同一视频第二跑）：模型丢 `[mm:ss]` 纪律、吐原生 ASR 裸秒（`[0.0]`/`[624.0]`）+ 每行 2-3 秒碎句——分层修：`normalizeAsrTimestamps` 转换行首裸秒（截位小数、>12h 拒收）；`transcriptEndSec` 独立识别**带小数**的行首裸秒（**勿喂归一化后的行**——mm:ss 截位把 4.6s 末戳变 4s、误触发 90% 守卫）；两份提示词禁裸秒 + 强制行粒度=一个完整句（5-20 秒语料），不是 ASR 一口气一行。校准与回归叙事：wiki ASR-and-Video-Analysis「Ark cache & keyframe calibration」。
- **千问AI平台 provider — built and removed (2026-08-31, user decision)**: a full qwen adapter (DashScope temp-file upload → OSS POST, filetrans async task API, Omni prompt-transcription, two-pass audiovisual merge) lived for one day; the browser→OSS POST link proved unstable in the user's network (ERR_CONNECTION_RESET on the bucket host while the dashscope API host was reachable) and the user chose to consolidate on the proven Ark/seed path. The complete implementation is preserved in git history (commits b555380 → de8ee2f) for resurrection. Facts worth keeping: qianwen vision models (qwen3.8-flash) take video as FRAMES ONLY and reject audio; only the Omni line accepts audio (combined A/V ~400s); `qwen-audio-3.0-asr-flash-filetrans` is the platform's recommended podcast/interview ASR (RESTful accepts oss:// temp URLs); GLM-5.3-flash's spec lists no audio modality either — "supports video" on these platforms never implies hearing it.
- **阿里云百炼 qwen provider — the OFFICIAL second ASR adapter (2026-10-06/07)**（裁决；完整 wire 细节与采纳/否决清单见 wiki ASR-and-Video-Analysis「qwen adapter」节）：非官方代理 qianwenai.com 死心；适配器合同 `{transcribeAudio, analyzeVideo, upload, poll, alive, fileCacheTtlSec, ping}`——供应商拥有自己整个文件生命周期。**Qwen wire 要点**：长音频唯一可行路 = `getPolicy` → OSS multipart（字段序固定、`file` 最后、成功=空 200）→ `oss://<key>` + `X-DashScope-OssResourceResolve: enable`（base64 <10MB ≈ 5min；公网 URL 403——与方舟 URL 直传死掉同根）；`oss://` 文件**绑定上传时的 model** 且 48h 死—— hence 缓存键追加 `|provider:model`，两条管线按**调用 model** 作用域化缓存；音频/视频 part 只准进 user message；omni 路径时间戳仍是提示词产出（共享 builder 从 ark 逐字节抽出）。**遗留探测项**：getPolicy 对 omni 模型的接受度、compatible-mode 是否钳 `max_tokens:65536`（ping 故意用生产值，让钳制在测试按钮现形而非转写中途）、⚠ 08-31 的 OSS 桶 host 曾在 API host 可达时 connection-reset——复发则 qwen 全路径被它劫持，兜底是 ark。**设置卡更名「多模态视频理解」**（用户拍板：ASR 只描述音频半边；内部键/存储保持 `asr` 前缀）。**已采纳质量杠杆**：SCREEN TEXT INTEGRITY 反幻觉规则、「绝不翻译/不改措辞」、omni chat 转写路径发 `reasoning_effort:'none'`（qwen3.8/3.5-omni 默认 xhigh，纯感知任务纯烧 token；**只发** `/^qwen3\.[85]-omni/`、**不发**精读路径）；**已评估否决**：use_multichannel / fps 15 / vl_high_resolution_images / 长媒体分段。⚠ **lockstep 教训在此复证**：prompt-builder 字符串链漏一个 `+` 被 ASI 截断（后半段规则静默消失），提示词纪律测试一轮即抓——**改 prompt-builder 必跑 lib-attach-asr + lib-asr-qwen**。
- **filetrans 异步任务族（2026-10-07）**：`*-filetrans`/`fun-asr*`/`paraformer*` 走录音文件转写任务 API 而非 chat（`X-DashScope-Async` → 轮询 → 拉 24h 签名 JSON）；提交体**按族分形状**（file_urls[] vs file_url vs 嵌套 output_result——三种都试）；映射到 `[mm:ss] [说话人N] text` 是**确定性**的（原生毫秒时间戳+说话人分离，取代提示词时间戳；90%/空洞守卫原样消费）。参数只留文档最小集；失败**响亮**（无静默兜底）。纯音频模型做不了视频精读：模式卡扣视频选项 + `qwenAnalyzeVideo` 掷指路错误兜底 + ping 单跳。内联热词：`parseAsrHotweights`（超限词剔除而非整单 400）→ filetrans `parameters.vocabulary` `{term:4}`，仅 filetrans 路径发。
- **ASR 默认值两次翻转（2026-10-07，用户拍板）+ 必须永存的遗留兼容守卫**：默认 provider=qwen、qwen 拆 `defaultModel`（转写 filetrans）/`defaultVideoModel`（精读 omni）——新用户开箱即得预期配置。**守卫**：`provider` 字段出现前的配置只有 ark 的 baseUrl/model 残迹——`attach-asr-pending.js` 在 ASR_DEFAULTS 合并**之前**按残迹推断 provider（`volces.com`/`doubao` → 'ark'），`asrAdapterFor(未知)` 也仍回退 ark（**回退 ≠ 默认**）——默认翻转绝不悄悄把 ark 时代用户指向 dashscope。
- **原始字节上传（2026-10-07 真机「上传太慢」）**：16kHz 单声道 WAV 是源流的 ~4×——转码的两份真实职责（ark 兼容、上传前时长核对）中**核对就是解码本身**；因此**「跳过转码」被否——只有编码+上传可跳**：`audioUploadFormat:'original'` 声明式上传（qwen 发原始 fMP4，ark 照旧 WAV），上传日志带 `| Ns (X.XMB/s)` 让「慢」自诊断。
- **playurl 过期自愈（`ASR_FRESH_URLS`）** — 裁决：B 站 m4s URL 是 deadline 签名的，页面开几小时后点 📎 时 URL 已过期、CDN 一律 403（注头/带 cookie 都没用）。自愈只走「重新拉一次 playurl」这一条路。踩过的两个坑：handler 曾返回 `{ok:true, data:{ok:true, streams}}` 而 background 的 `onMessage` 又包了一层，读错层使修复永不生效；googlevideo 即便给新鲜 pot URL 也会 403 纯扩展上下文下载，必须走 `downloadAudioBytes` 那条 DNR 注头（Referer+Origin+Cookie）。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **ASR-and-Video-Analysis-zh「playurl 过期自愈」**
- **YouTube injected func must NOT capture closure variables (countImages lesson, real production bug 2026-08-25)**: the first YouTube ASR implementation parsed `videoId` in `buildAsrPendingCtx`/`ASR_FRESH_URLS` and referenced it **inside** the `chrome.scripting.executeScript` injected `func` — but `executeScript` serializes `func` via `Function.prototype.toString()` and re-evaluates it in the page realm, so the closure variable is `undefined` there. Result: the fresh `/player` fetch got `undefined` videoId, returned no streams, `buildAsrPendingCtx` returned null, and the attach **silently fell through to the normal store path** — the user's "优先 ASR 解析字幕" (subtitleSource=asr) was ignored and the built-in transcript was used, with no error anywhere. The string-matching mocks in `attach-asr.test.mjs` (which inspect `func.toString()` and return canned results, never executing the func) could NOT catch this — only a real browser showed it. Fixed by parsing `vid` from `window.location.search` **inside** the func (same self-contained pattern as `activeYouTubeFetch`), and `test/attach-asr-closure.test.mjs` was added as a structural regression guard: it extracts every `__browsaFetchFreshYouTubeStreams` injected func from `background.js` source, re-evaluates it in a fresh `vm` context with ONLY a fake `window` (exactly what Chrome's serialization does), and asserts the func parses videoId from `window.location` + returns streams + does not reference `fn(videoId)`/`!videoId`. General rule: ANY injected `func` for `executeScript` must be self-contained — parse inputs from `window.location`/`window.*` inside the func body, never capture service-worker closure variables.
- **YouTube pot-URL capture (final attempt, 2026-08-25) — INFEASTBLE, concluded**: the page's REAL WEB player response carries `pot` (PO token) baked into each stream URL, because the actual player obtained it via its own botguard flow — unlike the ANDROID fresh POST (`fetchFreshYouTubeStreams`) which returns pot-LESS URLs that googlevideo 403s. `handlePlayerResponse` (the existing fetch/XHR interceptor) now also extracts audio-only adaptiveFormats with bare `url` via `readPlayerAudioStreams(playerData)`, caches the pot-bearing ones per videoId on `window.__browsaPlayerAudioCache`, and exposes `window.__browsaGetPlayerAudioStreams(videoId)`. `buildAsrPendingCtx`'s youtube branch (and `ASR_FRESH_URLS` youtube branch) PREFER these captured pot streams first, falling back to the ANDROID POST only when no pot capture exists. **VERDICT (after 4 live tests, 2026-08-25)**: still 403 — the downloads in the user's log were all `c=ANDROID` pot-less URLs, meaning the pot capture returned empty (the real player response's audio streams are signatureCipher-only / no bare url, or the captured pot is session-bound and a second fetch 403s). Combined with cat-catch (the proven Bilibili downloader) ALSO failing on googlevideo, the conclusion is definitive: **YouTube audio download is blocked by YouTube's PO-token anti-scraping (pot is session/connection-bound); yt-dlp only survives via a dedicated botguard pot-generation flow a browser extension cannot run. YouTube ASR is infeasible for now.** The code keeps the fail-open fallback (YouTube ASR failure → built-in transcript, which is present on essentially all YouTube videos and already works), Bilibili ASR is unaffected, and the sidepanel YouTube-403 toast now says explicitly 「YouTube 限制了音频下载，已回退使用自带字幕」. Do NOT invest further in YouTube audio download unless YouTube relaxes pot enforcement.
- **YouTube pot-URL 复验（2026-09-10）** — 裁决：403 未复现**不推翻**「扩展上下文直下 googlevideo 不可行」的结论，且样本 n=1。结论：**字幕覆盖率不能从这种环境取样，单个异常视频证明不了正常情况**。真实影响的落点：自制/无字幕视频（音乐/环境音/无对白无字）本来就抓不到内容，上传者可关闭字幕，Shorts/长尾常无字幕——对这类视频自有 ASR 是唯一路径，不是可有可无的兜底。
  → 完整机制/事故叙事：wiki **ASR-and-Video-Analysis-zh「YouTube pot-URL 复验」**
- **Tests**: `test/lib-attach-asr.test.mjs` (pure: `downloadAndUploadAudio`/`downloadAudioBytes`/`uploadBlobToArk`/`encodePcmToWav`/`resampleToMono`/`transcodeAudioBlob`/`pollFileStatus`/`transcribeAudio`/`formatAsrTranscript`/`extFromMime` — uses real `Blob` since Node's native `FormData` rejects plain objects; Web Audio mocked for transcode), `test/attach-asr.test.mjs` (background wiring: asr-pending detection incl. the `noTranscript` flag, transcript/disabled/no-audio-stream cases, `ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM` storage + `videoSrc` — now with a parallel **YouTube** set: youtube asr-pending with a fresh googlevideo URL + `asrPlatform:'youtube'` + empty `biliCookie`, youtube no-transcript normal path, youtube no-stream fallback, `ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM` platform=youtube stamps `videoSrc.platform='youtube'`), `test/lib-sidepanel-asr.test.mjs` (jsdom drives `onAttachPage` through the whole bilibili pipeline, mocking the Web Audio API + the Ark fetch; asserts the upload is the transcoded `audio.wav`, not the raw m4s), `test/lib-sidepanel-asr-youtube.test.mjs` (the same pipeline for youtube: asserts NO DNR rule is added, the download is a plain fetch with no bilibili Referer/Range, and `ATTACH_ASR_CONFIRM` carries `platform:'youtube'`), `test/youtube-streams.test.mjs` (added `fetchFreshYouTubeStreams`: ANDROID POST body, codecs/bandwidth extraction, skip non-audio + signatureCipher-only, never-throws). The bilibili wiring tests must mock `isPdfDocument`'s `document.contentType` probe to return `false` — a truthy fallback makes `extractActiveTab` treat the video page as a PDF and return `pdf-url` before the bilibili path runs.

### Detail-thread ("SUBCHAT") side conversations

Selecting text inside an assistant reply opens an inline card scoped to that excerpt (`lib/sidepanel/detail-thread.js`: `openDetailThread`, `findBlockAnchor`; `lib/handlers/subchat-handler.js`: `handleSubchat`/`handleSubchatAbort`, dispatched from `background.js`'s `SUBCHAT`/`SUBCHAT_ABORT` cases). Rules that are easy to accidentally violate when touching this code:

- **Never call `storage.appendToHistory`** for SUBCHAT — the side conversation never enters the main history or saved sessions, and closing the CARD discards it. (Since 2026-10-20, closing the SIDE PANEL is different: the open card is snapshotted to `chrome.storage.session` and restored — see the persistence bullet in the `detail-thread.js` section. It is still not main history.) Covered by a dedicated test in `test/subchat.test.mjs`.
- **Always stream via the provider's `apiStyle` (never `runsApiStream()`)** — no tool/approval flow needed for a side question, and reusing the main chat's Hermes session would mix it into the agent's main-task context. SUBCHAT dispatches to `chatStream`/`responsesStream`/`anthropicStream` by `apiStyle` (system prompt split into `instructions`/`system` for responses/anthropic).
- **`CHOICE_REQUEST_HINT` must stay out of SUBCHAT's system message** — it's a separate constant from `CAPABILITY_HINTS`, appended only to CHAT's `effectiveSystemPrompt`. SUBCHAT has no UI to parse/render the resulting buttons, so including the instruction leaks raw `CHOICE_REQUEST:{...}` JSON into replies.
- Rendering reuses `.msg.user`/`.msg.assistant` classes directly (not a parallel CSS ruleset) so it can't silently drift from the main chat's look — remember to add the `.done` class manually when a bubble finishes streaming, or the `.msg.assistant::after` blinking-cursor pseudo-element never goes away.

### ego-lite audit borrowings (citrolabs/ego-lite, 2026-09-09)

ego-lite is a Chromium fork where humans and agents co-use one browser (closed-source app; its repo ships only the open `ego-browser` CDP harness + agent skill). Different product class — Task Spaces ownership, kernel-level snapshot, CDP transport are NOT portable to a no-backend MV3 extension, and its visible signature features (Spaces, "experience accumulation") fall under the standing youtube-digest rule (techniques yes, signature features no). What was borrowed, techniques only:

- **Agent-executable setup prompt** (from its `references/install.md` pattern — instructions written for an agent to follow, not a human): the Agent Bridge card in `options.js` has a 「复制配置提示词」 button (`bridgeAgentSetupPrompt`, `_locales` en+zh) that copies a complete prompt the user pastes into their CLI agent, which then performs install → agents.json → serve → health-check itself and reports back only the bridge URL. Same block lives in `docs/guide/providers.html` (zh+en) and both READMEs link it. The prompt deliberately defers adapter-entry specifics to the bridge package's own `agents.example.json`/README so it can't drift. Ego's hard-stop discipline carried over too: the prompt ends with "if a step fails, stop and show the exact error — do not retry in a loop".
- **Per-site knowledge file**: `lib/content-scripts/SITES.md` — triage flow for "a site stops populating its cache", cross-site invariants (each paid for with a real bug: never touch signed URL params, GraphQL hash rotation, MAIN-world constraints, `var`-not-`const` for re-injected scripts), and one fixed-schema entry per site (hosts / intercept points / push message → cache field / product / fragilities / tests), shaped after ego's learnings packs. Update a site's entry in the same commit as any change to its script.

Audited and deliberately NOT adopted: ego's `scrollToBottomUntil(condition)` predicate scroll for extraction — `preExtractCleanup` already terminates on `newHeight <= lastHeight` (page stopped growing), which is the right predicate for "extract everything", and the deep-extract pass had already rejected adding content-condition termination as decoration. `CAPABILITY_HINTS` already satisfy its SKILL.md documentation discipline (anti-patterns named explicitly, every caveat carries its reason), so no churn there either. Direction-level finding: ego validates "agent acts on pages" (click/fill/type/upload are its core helpers) — but after a 2026-09-09/10 scenario review the user shelved the write/act direction entirely: the copy-paste baseline (write in the side panel, paste by hand) already covers single-blob writing, the surviving scenarios (multi-field forms / batch entry / page-feedback loops) are episodic or niche, and the call was 「做好太复杂了 — 就基于当前 browsa 的功能做好」. Do not re-propose generic page-write features; a future restart needs a concrete, real-usage form/batch flow copy-paste genuinely can't handle. (Its lightweight implementation blueprint — external-agent-as-brain via a structured chat-tail protocol, reusing the deep-extract safety skeleton and approval cards — is archived in the project roadmap notes for that day.)

### Testing

Tests use Node's built-in test runner. They mock the `chrome` global before importing extension modules. Tests are a mix of static-analysis/integration tests against `handle()` and real jsdom-based execution tests (import the real module, drive real DOM events, assert on real DOM output — using the actual vendored `marked`/`DOMPurify`/`katex`/`highlight.js` bundles, not stand-ins) for the `lib/sidepanel/*.js` modules. There is no real browser in any of this — jsdom stands in for the DOM. `sidepanel.js` itself has zero exports (it just calls `init()` at module load and only exposes behavior through DOM events), so its own tests (`test/lib-sidepanel-streaming.test.mjs`, `test/lib-sidepanel-resume-streaming.test.mjs`, `test/lib-sidepanel-status-dot.test.mjs`) load the real `sidepanel.html` into jsdom, mock the `chrome.*` surface `init()` needs, import the real `sidepanel.js`, and drive it via simulated clicks/keydowns/port messages — the same black-box approach a browser would use, since there's no exported function to call directly. The test suite has 1226 tests across 103 files. WebAssembly is the one exception to the "no real browser" rule above: unlike pdf.js (needs `DOMMatrix`/a real Worker), plain `WebAssembly.instantiate` runs directly in Node, so `test/lib-pdf-inspector-wasm.test.mjs` does real execution tests against the actual vendored `.wasm` binary instead of structural/mocked ones.


## 2026-09-23 全库评审批次（bug 修复 + 架构深化 + UI/UX）——给未来 agent 的不变量

**行为修复（B 批）**：
- **B1** edit&resend / regenerate 现在**移除旧 user 气泡**再由 onSend 重建（此前两条同 hidx 气泡争同一 storage 槽，删任一条都错位）。「一次发送只有一条 user 气泡」是不变量。
- **B3** pdf/office worker 客户端回包按 **requestId 匹配**（`matchPending` 单一实现，katex batchId 剧本）：带 id 的回包查无 pending 即丢弃（迟到回包不再把 A 文档结果串给 B）；无 id 回包保留 FIFO 兜底（仅测试替身走此路）。两个 worker 的每条 `self.postMessage` 必须带回 requestId——test/lib-worker-pending-match.test.mjs 有协议钉。
- **B4** 单条删除=storage 确认成功才移除 DOM（失败留在屏上）+ **两步 armed**（2s 内再击确认；Ctrl/Cmd+click 直删；同会话删除形态）。
- **B5** `storage.loadSession` 未命中返回 **-1**（0 是合法空会话长度）；调用侧只读内层 `res.data.ok`。
- **B6/B7** detail-thread：finalize/stopTurn 收尾在 `finally`（终渲抛错不再跳过清理）；`turnPort.onDisconnect` 自愈（SW 死亡按 stopTurn 语义就地收尾，`stopTurn({aborted:true})` 跳过 ABORT 发送）；waitTimer 气泡脱挂自停。jsdom 测试务必收尾回合（DONE/■/关卡），否则 SW_PING 泄漏拖死 runner。
- **B8** `quickbarAria` 键已补（en/zh）。
- **B9/U10/U11 i18n 扫尾**：图标按钮 title+aria-label 同源（`labelIconBtn`）；ICONS/内联 SVG 全部 `aria-hidden`；sessions 相对时间走 `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat`；`<html lang>` 跟随界面语言（init/watchUiLang 双点同步）；composer 占位符瘦身为「问点什么…/Ask anything…」、快捷键提示挪 `composerAria` 键。双 locale 新增键 30+，键集保持对称（lib-i18n 有钉）。
- **B2** options 多模型 chips 的 Enter 补 `isImeComposing` 防护（全库 Enter 提交口的最后一处漏网）。

**UI/UX 不变量（U 批）**：danger 确认框默认焦点在 Cancel（showConfirmDialog：role=alertdialog + 焦点陷阱 + 背景 inert，API 形状未变）；toast 容器 role=status/aria-live=polite；JS 平滑滚动一律 `smoothOrAuto()`（ui-utils 导出；jsdom 无 matchMedia 时退化 smooth）；会话行/字幕行键盘可达（Enter/Space=激活、F2=重命名）；追问卡调高 handle 可聚焦（↑/↓ 20px）；浅色对比度 token 三件套 `--tier-*` / `--success` light 重映射 / `--fg-faint`（theme.css 双主题）。

**Hermes /v1/runs 请求形状订正（2026-09-24 真机 422，按服务器源码实锤）**：agent 管线的严格层只认部件 `text`/`image_url`/`file`——`input_text`/`input_image` 只是入口归一化白名单（api_server.py `_TEXT_PART_TYPES` 等）宽容接受的**别名**，而 `/v1/runs` 的解析（api_server_runs.py）不过归一化、部件原样递进管线，别名在严格层被 422。browsa 侧契约自此：**当前轮图片部件用规范 `text`/`image_url`（`image_url:{url}` 对象形）；`conversation_history` 的 content 一律字符串**（多模态 entry 扁平化为文本 + `[image]` 标记——服务端 `str(entry["content"])` 会把数组 repr 成 Python 垃圾、base64 灌进提示词，历史图片在当前 /v1/runs 无通道，09-12「历史图片被识别」的实测结论已过时）。`/v1/responses` 渠道经 `partStyle:'responses'` 保留 input_* 多模态原样（该 API 原生拼写）；chat/anthropic/opencode/bridge 不受影响（opencode/bridge 走 agent-turn.js 独立管线）。回归钉在 chat-handler-internals（含「base64 不泄漏」断言）。
**架构深化记录**（C1 llm-client SSE 骨架、C3 finishBubble、C4 agent-stream-session、C5 prompt-assembly、C7 site-cache-store、C9 builders→message-builder）已并入上文对应小节。
- **C2 行为测试替换源码 pin**：`test/chat-rebuild-channels.test.mjs`（新，18 例）以「真 handleChat + stub SSE + 捕获请求体」覆盖 continuation/timestamp-rewrite/overflow 三条重建通道的**每 wire 形状**，含 anthropicSystem 回归钉（无状态 turn 不得抛 ReferenceError）；chat-handler-internals 的 3 组源码正则 pin 相应退役（WHEN-gate 字面量 pin 与 `_stCont.acc` 针照旧保留）。⚠ mock 铁律：内存版 chrome.storage mock 必须 `structuredClone` 读写——真 chrome.storage 每读反序列化新对象，返回活引用会让 `appendToHistory` 的原地 push 污染 sendHistory（C2 实测踩过）。

## 2026-10-01 UI/UX 界面整理

顶栏用模型/Agent 首行和服务商次行显示当前选择，但实际交互仍是原生 `#provider`，完整 option 名称/状态及 dataset.model/display 不变；视觉 caption `aria-hidden` 且 pointer-events:none，系统强制配色回到原生文字。设置和会话管理为全局入口，必须常驻顶栏，不随页面类型隐藏或收进更多菜单。搜索/多选原 ID 保留，移入 `#toolbar-tools` 原生 details：Esc/外点/焦点离开/动作完成收起，不声明 ARIA menu（内部仍是普通按钮）。快捷栏自动换行，保持折叠键。HTML 和 Intl 的语言统一经 i18n.uiLocale（存储 pt 对应 pt-BR，auto 按浏览器匹配已提供词典，其他回落 en），不要再写 zh/en 二分或把 auto 放进 HTML lang。后续已完成七语 v7 视频/海报和 demo-v16 GIF，均使用整理后的顶栏和图标；当前分发改为七语 YouTube 链接。旧 v4/v10 制作产物仅作历史输入，不能作为当前素材。会话重命名保存开始时禁用输入框与同行重命名按钮，结果返回后恢复按钮，避免 Tab 后再次激活已提交编辑器而丢字。


### Historical Promo v6 browser-first production (superseded by v7, 2026-10-01)
The historical v6 promo is a standalone Remotion project `.agents/skills/promo-video/video-v5` driven by `scripts/copy-v5.json`, `scripts/browser-v5.html`, and `scripts/capture5.mjs`. Production sidepanel is captured via the preview shim at 2×; browser frame/player are compositional demo assets, replies/Agent saved result simulated, interaction events verified. Do not claim real file writing or online latency. Camera crops derive from capture.json DOM bounds; timestamp's stored bounds must explicitly target 07:42 rather than generic first .browsa-ts. Preview bridge seeds use models URL list + bridgeAgents + endpointPing to match current production configuration.

52s, 1080p30, seven localized MP4 + no-BGM MP4 (SFX retained, no voice), posters and demo-v12 20s GIF. Keep src/timeline.ts the sole shot/SFX owner; src/workbench.ts imports it. No-BGM audio is independently rendered from bgm:false same timeline, muxed with main video stream to guarantee visual equality. Original procedural BGM and source-attributed SFX avoid orphan-license music. User chose no spoken narration: voice defaults false and render props/workbench omit voice; keep seven-language UI/captions. Current filming changes no production UI: guide screenshots and banners remain current; GIF/video both refreshed. YouTube source remains old until user replaces it.

Promo v6 retains the historical video-v5 source directory and v5 base captures. capture-render6.mjs films real KaTeX/code, Mermaid, ECharts; capture-workflow6.mjs films the native transcript drawer462s seek, multiple Agent options/change, three saved sessions/restore, copied Agent ID. prepare-assets.mjs deterministically copies seven textures, uses measured bounds for crop PNGs and emits workflow-bounds.json. Call prepare-assets after recapture, before concurrent renders. Card-stack, row-embed, line-carry-transition and logo-shrink-wordmark-lockup were adapted from exact shotcraft demos. CLI continuation panel is explicitly DEMO illustration of the documented ID mechanism; never claim one-click launch/live terminal. timeline-travel/pill-slot-cycle considered but excluded. Gallery reference movies unavailable, no dynamic-equivalence claim. Render uses two shared voice-free WAVs; delete them or --refresh-audio after timeline/music/SFX edits; never reuse stale audio. Wait for all7 renders before verify. Native session restore capture waits for hidden drawer state=attached (default Playwright visibility would time out).


### Promo v7 Ink Press production (2026-10-01)

Maintained workflow: `.agents/skills/promo-video/SKILL.md`, `references/production-v7.md` (capture/freeze/render/verify) and `references/maintenance.md` (iteration/partial update). These replace the former append-only v7 timing notes. Active project `video-v7`; `src/timeline.ts` owns shots/SFX, `src/workbench.ts` imports it. Current four-chapter main: read articles → watch videos → switch Agent inside browsa → return to Agent, using Codex as the concrete continuation example. Session management bridges chapter04; Transformer is the technical article/diagram example, and the fictional AI/work interview is separate. Existing README slogans remain authoritative.

Native UI is captured at2×; renderer features remain inside the sidepanel. Accepted formulas/DOT reuse `store-assets/promo/native-live/actual-history.json`, rendered by the current unpacked extension; whole-panel composition is not a whole-window recording. No new model call is necessary for UI recapture. Main CLI is DEMO; Chinese growth/live proof is separate. Carry-context requires explicit user choice and verified next CHAT.backfill=true, never silent automatic synchronization.

Asset order matters: capture base/workflow and native first; current prepare-assets automatically invokes prepare-native; capture-conversation-final7 then overwrites final BYOK/follow-up/Agent screenshots, and its reports must be merged into src/conversation.json so cursor bounds follow changed UI. Re-running prepare-assets afterward can restore old conversation pictures. Freeze public before rendering. Missing gitignored proof/assets require recovery, not invented replacement evidence.

Current Cat Walk audio has no narration. build-audio must resample click source to48kHz BEFORE sample-based adelay; source music phase and final output offset stay separate. Use swiftshader/concurrency2 for main renders, full encoded blank-frame scan in addition to decode, picture-stream hashes for music/SFX-only pairs, current audio measurement, sampled workbench parity and clean-context independent review. Title-only patch-chapter is specific to unchanged chapter04 beats108–112 plus8-frame transitions; it preserves original audio. Other timeline revisions recalculate ranges.

Screenshot/banner/GIF/video impact must each be documented. GIF content changes increment demo-vN; seven README/landing references follow their language outputs. Film production does not authorize Git operations, uploads or publication.

### 2026-10-01 发布包与隐私披露

打包排除全部 `README.*.md`，包括未来语言；翻译文档不是扩展运行资产。七语包内名称/简短说明按 CWS 75/132 字符限制核对，回归测试直接运行真实打包脚本。无遥测/无开发者后台不等于不处理数据：隐私政策区分本地保存、配置服务认证和推理、站点接口/媒体获取、外部回复图片与结构数据库；不得再写“全部请求只到自选端点”或“完全不上传”。

**设置入口外置（2026-10-01 用户裁决）**：设置与会话为顶栏常驻入口；更多仅放搜索/多选。本次仅移设置按钮，未影响既有片中的演示操作或功能表达，按上述通用素材影响规则保留 v7 视频/demo-v16 GIF，留待下一轮宣传素材升级同步。指南的可辨顶栏截图和七语 README/中英操作指南本轮同步；既有 YouTube 入口保留。

**清空消息图标（2026-10-01 用户纠正）**：输入区 `#clear` 执行的是确认后的 CLEAR_HISTORY，使用垃圾桶图标，不能以「对话加号」暗示创建并保留旧会话。七语提示为清空消息；沿用 `newChatTitle` 字典键以兼容现有标记。清空后无撤销入口，文档不得承诺 5 秒撤销。`test/lib-sidepanel-ui-prefs.test.mjs` 核对实际按钮图形、七语可访问名称与确认前后行为；`test/fixtures/clear-messages.svg` 是已确认的图形基线，尺寸变化不锁定。修改基线须先核对动作语义，不能为让测试通过而随产品 SVG 一起盲改。
